| Current recorded Review packet slots | `review-evidence.ts` current packet/recorded source adapter registry and its actual slot formula; reuse its adapter id only for that registry's own projections |
| Recorded grade | RunService's existing grade source owner `reviewSources`; exact `derived.grade.move_quality@2` source factory/route |
| Original structural/window population | Same owner's `reviewPopulation` and the existing recorded semantic window owner; exact literal per-family projections from their original retained results |
| Recorded measurements | Same owner's `reviewMeasurements`; each original measurement's own exact projection, never a grade or invented Story adapter |
| Single-line recorded objectives | Same owner's `reviewObjectives`; `run.record.objective_transition@2`, not a fabricated two-branch comparison |
| Other effective ordinary modules | `moduleQuerySourceRoutes` and their existing actual source context (installed opening, authored ledger, shapes, recorded comparison or explorer); the row joins that actual route's exact output projection |

The literal plan must be total/set-equal over the demanded current callable image before
collection; a missing owner or mismatched output refuses instead of fabricating registry
membership. Source plan rows describe dispatch/metadata only and cannot mint evidence. Do not
activate accepted-but-unsupplied routes, broaden module acceptances or hide a missing source
behind the twelve Story adapters. Reuse the real finite `ReviewAdapterState` vocabulary, including
`pending` and `not_yet_scheduled`; neither becomes `honest_empty`/`no_witness`. Validate source,
invocation, projection and subject against that original owned job plan. No nonexistent generic
`ModuleSourceReceipt` or unrestricted reason string is a wire contract. Failed/cancelled pages
report only actually observed slots; unreached subjects never acquire fabricated receipts.

The actual outer Review reply is exactly the existing nine-field `ModuleQueryPage`
`{protocol, runId, timing, subjectNodeId, decision, requestedConfigDigest,
effectiveConfigDigest, packets, suppressions}` plus required `review`. `subjectNodeId` is the
validated focus when supplied, otherwise the selected branch tip; it never limits enumeration.
Keep existing per-packet disclosure receipts; there is no new top-level disclosure or
sourceReceipts field. All source metadata lives inside `review`.

The Review metadata arm is exactly `{jobId, branchId, prefixDigest, pageSubjectIds,
nextCursor, complete, state, elapsedMs, remainingBudgetMs, candidates, sourceReceipts, failure}`.
`state` is `collecting | complete | cancelled | stale | failed`. `failure` is null except failed,
where it is `budget_exhausted | source_failed`. Collecting requires non-null nextCursor,
`complete:false` and `packets:[]`; complete requires null cursor, `complete:true` and final
normal packets; every terminal noncomplete state requires null cursor and `packets:[]`.
Elapsed/remaining values use the same absolute clock and remaining is clamped at zero.
`candidates` is empty on final/terminal pages; source receipts retain honest upstream states.
Collection-complete means all planned slots have been observed, not all upstream analysis ready:
existing pending/not-ready Review controls and their disabled primary action remain truthful.
Unauthorized/unknown-handle errors expose none of this metadata.

**Finalization and proof.** After all original subjects, merge retained original evidence in
canonical subject order, deduplicate shared evidence identity and run ordinary reducers once.
Apply Story's existing top-eight convention once over the complete pool. Review Map's distinct
zero-to-three-moment rule stays distinct. Assert F1/presentation brands before normal presentation
serialization, then retire the job; the strict client accepts only the normal registered
components. Never rank/truncate per page, render progress references, broaden a module's source
acceptances or claim adoption merely because a plan names a source. Unavailable/pending sources
retain their existing presentation state. The one-pass frozen-prefix oracle must equal final
paged packet, disclosure and presentation bytes for limits1,2,32, inherited branches, repeated
FENs and over32-entry paths. Compare the same run/decision/configuration/subject and suppressions;
exclude only job identity and per-page progress metadata (`jobId`, `pageSubjectIds`, `nextCursor`,
`elapsedMs`, `remainingBudgetMs`, interim candidate/source receipt slices). Neither evidence nor
its source identity, complete final source coverage, ordering or content is excluded. Permanent
negatives cover wrong owner/session/config/branch/focus, mutation/rewind/revoke after yields,
foreign and duplicate cursors, absolute idle expiry, real cancellation, synchronous overrun,
capacity, cleanup and release of retained pools. Actual authenticated route, strict receiver and
selected Review progress/cancel/retry/final journeys remain required for D4228 completion.

Before an assembler or provider runs, the boundary parser applies four closed validations
([[D1869]]): `selectedSquare` passes chessops `parseSquare` and is scoped to the authoritative node;
`candidateUci` passes `parseUci`, canonical round-trip and membership in that node's exact legal
move set after promotion normalization; `checkpointId` resolves to an open checkpoint occurrence
on the subject boundary; `hintRung` parses through the sealed `HintRung` vocabulary and is no higher
than the server-derived effective ceiling. Generation must be a non-negative safe integer. Invalid
square, malformed/illegal/stale UCI, foreign/closed checkpoint, over-ceiling rung and stale decision
stamp are distinct typed refusals. A browser-normalized value is never server authority.

The server resolves the run, branch and exact **decision stamp**. There is no invented run revision
([[D1858]]). The stamp is derived from existing authoritative
bytes as `{ eventHeadSeq, cursor: { branchId, nodeId }, disclosureBoundarySeq }`, where
`eventHeadSeq` is the last event's contiguous `seq` and `disclosureBoundarySeq` is the exact open
boundary occurrence or `null`. Its canonical digest is recomputed by the server for every request.
A rewind, fork, branch/node change, disclosure close or a return to the same node after another
event therefore produces a different stamp.

The route returns a closed `ModuleQueryPage` carrying `{ runId, decision, subjectNodeId, timing,
generation?, requestedConfigDigest, effectiveConfigDigest, suppressions, sourceReceipts, packets,
disclosureReceipt, review? }`; `decision` contains the stamp fields and digest. `review` is absent
outside Review and otherwise carries `{prefixDigest, pageSubjectIds, nextCursor, complete,
elapsedMs, remainingBudgetMs}`. `packets` is final module output only when `complete`; interim
review candidates remain in the sealed review-page arm and cannot occupy a learner seat.
Each packet carries module id, timing, budget receipt,
`noveltyAbstained`, typed empty state and the sealed-component wire items owned by
`evidence-presentation`. F1 brands are process-local and do not cross JSON; the server serializes
only after brand assertions and the client accepts only the strict component parser. Extra fields,
unknown projections/components, digest mismatch and a subject not belonging to the run are typed
refusals.

Every non-empty response also carries a server-created `ModuleDisclosureReceipt` binding the run,
decision-stamp digest, subject, timing, module id, requested/effective configuration digests and
the ordered presentation-component digests. Post-commit, checkpoint and Review receipts join their
existing durable boundary events. Pre-commit threat/sight and at-commit warning receipts are the
narrow [[D1866]] exemption: they are process/request receipts returned before presentation, not run
events, because the selected square and staged uncommitted candidate cease to exist on reload.
They may prove request/response correlation and prevent unsolicited rendering; they **may not**
satisfy novelty, longitudinal history, hint-stage credit, campaign rewards or later reconstruction.
`intent-presets` §6 must be amended in the same author wave so its reopen condition permits this
exemption only for `pre_commit | at_commit` and still requires durable boundaries everywhere else.
If a later feature needs either transient disclosure after the request, D5's durable per-learner
store becomes mandatory rather than silently promoting this receipt.

Post-commit ordering is load-bearing: `DrillSessionController.move` receives the authoritative
`MutationResult.run`, queries with the learner `subjectNodeId` from that result, and only then calls
`#playOpponentIfNeeded`. Querying the active cursor afterwards would describe the automatic reply.
Pre-commit and checkpoint calls occur only on the explicit selection/hint gesture; Review queries
on surface load and replay selection. The cache key is the complete request plus decision-stamp
digest and effective-config digest. Before publishing an asynchronous result, the server and
client both compare the current stamp with the request stamp; a late result is a typed stale result
and never renders. A mutation, rewind/fork, selection generation, disclosure close or source
availability change invalidates the relevant entry.
#### 2.6 Steps 5–6 — seat, client side

`apps/web/src/lib/module-seats.ts` (new) maps packets to seats; `ModuleSeat.svelte` (new) renders
one seat in the three states `play-composition.md` §4.1 defines (collapsed badged row, expanded
card, empty-quiet); `CompanionRegion.svelte` (new) is the queue that owns the open/close protocol
(at most one expanded seat; expanding one collapses the previous; companion-region internals only,
never stage layout). `DrillScreen.svelte`'s existing `#run-support-region` — today an ad-hoc
`.evidence-seat` holding a reveal button, the guard prompt and the sight caption — becomes that
region's host. Seat expansion state is component state, persisted nowhere, which is why this RFC
stores no preference.

#### 2.7 One staged-move protocol for all five input modes

`blunder_prevention` is inserted after the shared `BoardInputController` produces one exact UCI
and after promotion choice, but before `onMove`. Click, drag and touch already converge at
Chessground's `after`; keyboard-grid and text converge on the same `BoardInputResult.moveUci`.
There is one controller-level state machine, never five interceptors:

```ts
type StagedMoveState =
  | { kind: "idle"; generation: number }
  | { kind: "checking"; generation: number; mode: MoveInputMode; uci: string; restore: InputRestoreReceipt }
  | { kind: "warning"; generation: number; mode: MoveInputMode; uci: string; restore: InputRestoreReceipt }
  | { kind: "unavailable"; generation: number; mode: MoveInputMode; uci: string; restore: InputRestoreReceipt }
  | { kind: "committing"; generation: number; mode: MoveInputMode; uci: string; restore: InputRestoreReceipt };
```

If the module is ineffective, the candidate bypasses the query and commits normally. An honest
empty response commits once and emits no all-clear. A concrete risk holds the exact candidate for
`revise` or exact-once `confirm`. Source/server failure is shown as unavailable and also requires
explicit confirmation; it is never called safe. `revise` restores active square, origin, input
focus and text value. A new gesture or revise increments generation, so late responses are ignored.
The board announces a committed move only after the mutation succeeds, not when a candidate is
staged. A 5-mode × risk/empty/unavailable × confirm/revise matrix, promotion-before-stage and stale
generation fixtures are one acceptance unit.

### §3 — The five regions and the seat classes

`design/05` §2 names five regions inside a run and says what each generically *is*.
`play-composition.md` §4 binds the closed seat-class vocabulary to composition regions. This RFC
consumes both and adds only the module→region assignment for the eleven.

| region (`design/05` §2) | seat class | modules | today |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Board and objective | `board_input` | `rules_floor` | the board's own affordance layer — legal-destination dots from `board-input.ts:195-199` through chessground's `movable.dests` (`Chessboard.svelte:145-151`). Paint, zero layout |
| 2. Timeline | `timeline` | `review_map` | `Timeline.svelte` renders the strip and already carries checkpoint dots, authored `A` chips, guard `G` chips, shape chips and pivotal dots |
| 3. Branch rail | — | none | a **structural** seat (`play-composition.md` §4.4), not a module; `BranchRail`/`GroupPanel` keep it |
| 4. Assistance and evidence rail | `board_adjacent` (1) and `rail` (7) | `blunder_prevention` in the reserved head slot; `sight_on_request`, `threat_radar`, `postcommit_nudge`, `structure_nudge`, `theory_breadcrumb`, `guided_hint`, `compare_coach` as rail seats | `#run-support-region` behind the `Support` compact tab; three of the seven have ad-hoc content, four have none |
| 5. Session and role controls | — | none | structural; topbar/session chrome |
| (outside the play composition) | `explicit_surface` | `full_inspector` | the separate Inspector surface `play-composition.md` §2.4 already landed |

Three points that are contract rather than layout:

**3.1 The one board-adjacent cue is a priority contract, not a geography.** `module-contract.ts:170`
and `:198` permit exactly one `board_adjacent` module and assign it to `blunder_prevention`;
[[D876]] ruled the cue is a companion-region card and the board stays pixel-identical. It owns the
reserved head slot and is the only module that may displace the expanded seat.

**3.2 The queue is the growth answer at every viewport**, unchanged from `play-composition.md`
§4.1: one expanded seat, the rest collapsed to badged rows, count badges retiring the L2 topbar
counter. `on_request` seats carry **no count before a request** — the row is the door, never a
claim of pending facts.

**3.3 `review_map`'s seat class needs one amendment, stated rather than fudged.**
`ModuleSeatClass` is a single value (`module-contract.ts:73`), and both `learner-modules.md` §4 and
`play-composition.md` §4 write `review_map` as *"timeline + explicit_surface"*, which is not
declarable. This RFC declares `seatClass: "timeline"` and amends both rows to read **"a timeline
mark that opens the review surface"** — one seat class, no expressiveness lost, and it matches what
ships: `Timeline.svelte`'s pivotal dot already opens a dialog that offers the review surface.

**3.4 The play-column placement of the inspector becomes non-conformant**, which is the point:
declaring `explicit_surface` makes `DrillScreen.svelte:1133-1196`'s in-play inspector modal a
contract violation that `play-composition.md`'s Discharge D2 relocates.

**3.5 One clause is owed to `play-composition.md` §5.1.** Its vocabulary law confines raw eval
numerals to the `explicit_surface`, and `review_map` is `timeline`-seated while its own surface
renders recorded trajectories (`GameStoryScreen.svelte:52`: *"Recorded trajectory: {before} →
{after} cp"*). The law needs a review-surface clause — a run past `outcome.reached` discloses under
every policy — and it is that RFC's to write. Proposed ledger row; Discharge D3.

### §4 — What happens to each ad-hoc precursor

Nothing shipped is dropped silently. Eight modules have precursors; three have none.

#### 4.1 `rules_floor` — **the precursor becomes the module's renderer, unchanged**

`board-input.ts` (437 lines) and `Chessboard.svelte` are the richest thing in the client and none
of it is contract-visible: the parallel semantic grid (`Chessboard.svelte:332-359`), per-square
aria labels (`board-input.ts:213-236`), the full keyboard model including double-Escape grid exit
(`Chessboard.svelte:252-264`), fifteen live-region announcement strings (`board-input.ts:156-412`),
the promotion dialog, the text-move entry with ambiguity detection (`:169-184`), the chessground
bounds repair (`:198-211`), and focus restoration after a move. **All of it stays exactly as it
is.** The module declaration adds one thing and removes none: the dots become a *declared* module
whose `ceilings.visibleBoardParity` is `true`, so `accessible-board-input`'s grid can never widen
what `showDests` withholds ([[D659]]). `accepts: { kind: "none" }` is why: the affordance layer is
interaction, not evidence.

#### 4.2 `sight_on_request` — **precursor becomes the renderer; its budget tightens 16 → 1, and the census moves**

Kept: all 27 grounded sentence templates (`structural-sentences.ts:8-33`) with their scope
disclaimers, which become the registered per-projection renderers; the `aria-live="polite"` caption
seat; the honest downgrade line *"No disclosed evidence exists here; structural sight remains
available."* (`DrillScreen.svelte:978`); the post-commit arm (§1.1).

Changed: the shipped consumer's `budget: { maxFacts: 16, maxForms: 2 }`
(`evidence-catalog.ts:878`) becomes the module's `maxFacts: 1` with §3 selection. This is the
measured defect the contract retires — R3 recorded median 2 / p95 9 / max 11 captions and up to 19
marks per square gesture with no eligibility, no selection and no budget. **The full census is not
deleted; it moves** to `full_inspector` (leak L7's declared destination).

Repaired: the shipped trigger is pointer-only — chessground's `select` event sets
`selectedSquare` (`DrillScreen.svelte:892`) and the keyboard grid path does not. `design/05` §3-forms
as amended requires *"input semantics must be equivalent for touch, hover/pointer and
keyboard/assistive use"*, so the keyboard activation path sets the same state. Criterion A11.

#### 4.2a Draft D3578 amendment — selected-square views, not whole-board populations

**Author: codex, 2026-10-09. Independent acceptance required before implementation.** This
section proposes the narrow repair requested by `planning/learner-modules/registry-return.md`.
The six predecessor controls still pass because production still has the gap. The additional
disposable model checks proposed selection over real sealed readings; it is neither a new source
authority nor proof of the shipping query, parser, renderer or input journey.

**Boundary choice.** Do not add an arbitrary `context` argument to every presentation adapter.
Do not change a sealed position reading's payload or advertise a partial reading under its old ID.
Create four explicit selected-square derivations through the existing sole value-factory/route
boundary. Their payload owns the selected square, exact full FEN, literal admitted source and the
computed selected result. Adapters continue to accept one admitted value; the selection now exists
*inside that value*, rather than being inferred from array order or supplied as prose.

| New independently versioned projection | Its single declared input | Selected meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `derived.sight.legal_moves@1` | `rules.mobility.reading.legal_moves@1` | Complete exact move rows and distinct semantic destinations of this actual-turn origin |
| `derived.sight.square_control@1` | `rules.square.reading.control@1` | Both colors' inbound controller identities at this target, separating geometric and clone-legal modes |
| `derived.sight.pawn_contacts@1` | `rules.pawn.reading.contacts@1` | This pawn's retained state and directly incident contacts, locks and connected-passer pairs |
| `derived.sight.castling_rights@1` | `rules.castling.reading.rights@1` | Rights of this king, or the current right carried by this exact castling rook |

Each declaration has exactly that input in `dependsOn` and `derivation.inputs`, and retains the
actual source item, not a caller-labelled reference. Grounding, exactness, confidence and conventions
inherit the input without promotion; in particular, square control does not become exact
actual-turn mobility. Use one catalog-local derived producer for these four operations; no new
collector, provider, global version head, schema or database migration is claimed. This amendment
retains `tabiya-claims: none`. The new projection IDs are proposed, not registered at this checkpoint.

**Factory inputs and refusal.** Each sole route accepts exactly `{source, fen, selectedSquare}`.
`source` must be a genuine value-authority-sealed item of the row's exact input projection/version.
The factory canonicalizes the full supplied FEN, requires exact equality with `source.payload.fen`
(including turn, rights, en-passant and clocks), validates `[a-h][1-8]`, computes the result itself,
and mints through the existing `evidence-factories.ts` authority. It accepts no output operands,
ancestry, caption, callback, renderer or caller-selected strength. Copied/forged source objects,
wrong input families, cross-position inputs and invalid selections fail before construction.
Original source payloads/receipts remain byte-identical. Full Inspector keeps its original
position-wide input, bindings, adapters and meaning; selected slices do not replace its populations.

Declare `fen`, `selectedSquare` and `result` as selected projection operands; the full input is
literal derivation ancestry, not a competing square-selection authority. For these four facts,
`ModuleFact.subjectSquare` is exactly `payload.selectedSquare`; never use the generic
`firstSquare` walk through a retained parent population. Identity retains the frame and selected
result, so different selections or positions cannot deduplicate as the same requested answer.

The four available payloads share only this frame; the result type is projection-specific,
not `unknown`, a generic operand dictionary or an arbitrary member list:

```ts
type SelectedSight<S, R> = Readonly<{
  fen: string;
  selectedSquare: SquareName;
  source: DeclaredEvidence<S>;
  result: R;
}>;
// Types on the right are the existing runtime interfaces, not rewritten source schemas.
type SelectedMoves = Readonly<{
  turn: Color;
  piece: ExactLegalMoveMap["pieces"][number]["piece"];
  moves: readonly ExactLegalMove[];
  destinations: readonly SquareName[];
}>;
type SelectedControl = readonly Readonly<{
  color: Color;
  pseudo: readonly SquareController[];
  legal: { readonly kind: "available"; readonly controllers: readonly SquareController[] }
    | { readonly kind: "unavailable"; readonly reason: "invalid_turn_clone" };
}>[];
type SelectedPawns = Readonly<{
  state: PawnContactsReading["passed"][number];
  contacts: PawnContactsReading["contacts"];
  locks: PawnContactsReading["locks"];
  connectedPassedPairs: PawnContactsReading["connectedPassedPairs"];
}>;
type SelectedRights = Readonly<{
  color: Color;
  wings: readonly Readonly<{
    wing: "kingside" | "queenside";
    retained: boolean;
    rookSquare: SquareName | null;
  }>[];
}>;
```

Unavailable/inapplicable route results are closed factory result arms, not newly minted chess
facts: use exactly the reasons below, retaining control-family availability even for no witness.
The sole factory returns these arms; adapters cannot manufacture empty/availability verdicts.
`no_witness` below names the module state, not a new dispatcher token: use the existing
`not_matched` route arm. The control absence reason is `no_available_controller` and retains a
closed per-color `legalAvailability` array (`available`, or `unavailable/invalid_turn_clone`).
Carry that typed metadata through source flattening and the exact module-empty parser instead
of discarding it. Only available selected values are minted; an unavailable calculation is not
a derived fact asserting zero controllers. These absence arms retain the same selected request
and disclosure lifetime as an available answer.

**Selection semantics, closed per family:**

- **Moves:** an empty origin is `no_witness(empty_square)`; an opponent origin is
  `unavailable(not_side_to_move)`, not a turn-flipped legal map. An actual-turn piece retains its
  *whole* row, including zero moves. Every `uci`, `from`, `to`, `role` and optional `promotion`
  survives. Destinations are the sorted unique `to` values, never origins or UCI suffixes.
  Four promotions to one square remain four choices. Castling retains king-to-rook UCI separately
  from the king landing square, including a Chess960 landing equal to its origin. An available
  empty row means “no legal moves for this piece”; it is not a safety verdict or missing evidence.
- **Control:** the selection is always a **target**, empty or occupied. Retain exactly that
  cell's complete controller list from each color's pseudolegal map and, independently, its
  clone-legal map or `invalid_turn_clone` state. Never flatten the two modes or render a missing
  clone as zero. No controllers gives `no_witness` with the availability states retained, not
  “safe”. The ordinary label is control/geometry; clone-legal detail discloses the supplied
  convention (color-flipped turn, en-passant cleared), not actual-turn move legality.
- **Pawns:** an empty/non-pawn selection is `no_witness(not_a_pawn)`. A pawn retains its own
  `passed` row, including the boolean, blockers and `protectedBy`; contacts retain either endpoint
  equal to the selected square, locks retain either endpoint, and connected pairs retain either
  member. Do not recursively pull in another pawn's population or discard a passer because it
  has no contact. These facts assert relations, not strength, profitable capture or strategy.
- **Castling:** a selected king retains both rights of its color, including `false`; the source
  FEN supplies exact current king/rook identities. A selected rook retains only wings whose
  current castling rook equals that square. Do not assume a/h rook origins in Chess960, or assign
  a rook a historical right after that right has disappeared. Other selections are
  `no_witness(not_castling_piece)`. A retained right does not mean castling is legal now; the
  existing legality reading remains separately scoped. A rights snapshot supplies no cause of loss.

**Query and learner choice.** Extend only the selected Sight request with an optional, closed
`sightFacet: auto | patterns | moves | control | pawns | castling`. It is invalid without requested
Sight and a valid selected square, and at timings without a selected-square subject. This is a
request-local view, not a persistent preference, preset, permission or relevance ranking. Omission
means `auto`: a pawn selects Pawns, a king Castling, another actual-turn piece Moves, otherwise
Control. Patterns explicitly retains the existing bounded structural selection. These defaults
are part of this **draft proposal**, not a new owner ruling or an implemented default.

An opened Sight card offers the same compact view chooser on desktop and phone; ordinary entry
does not require configuring producers. A view change asks the existing query for that family
only, with the same finalized assistance and exact decision/subject. It never accumulates facts
from the previous view or opens a whole-board census. Explain an inapplicable view instead of
silently substituting another family. Withheld/unavailable/no-witness remain distinct.

Extend the closed request parser, request identity/current-response comparisons, disclosure
subject and exact client parser together to retain the **requested and resolved facet**. The
server resolves against the authorized `ModuleSubject.fen`, not a client board snapshot. Its
selected derivation FEN/square must match that subject before reduction/admission. Existing
post-commit requests use the incoming move's **semantic landing square** from the shared exact
move authority, not `moveUci.slice(2, 4)` (which names the rook on castling). The current
`subjectFor` and ancestor-square slices require this repair; the existing exact-mobility
identity/destination distinction already specifies it. A client cannot add a different square
under that timing. Pre-commit uses the validated requested square. Changing facet,
selection, position, access, role, help or leaving/closing retires pending and delivered output.
Facet does not change the decision's assistance ceiling or grant a module/form.

The exact source/operation image must now declare original acquisition **and** selected
derivation; preflight all required bindings before reading either. Update the module accepts,
source routes, operation resolution and pair execution from that one image. The four whole-board
bindings leave **Sight only** in favor of their selected successors; their Inspector use remains.
Do not add `sightScope` branches returning every square for the original populations.

**Complete legality versus bounded explanations.** There is still at most **one fact** in Sight.
For the exact `module.sight_on_request@1 × derived.sight.legal_moves@1` pair only, use a declared
complete-legality envelope `{maxFacts: 1, maxWords: 96, maxMarks: 64, maxArrows: 0}`. All other
Sight pairs retain `{1, 30, 6, 1}`. This is an explicit per-pair exception, not a raised module-wide
setting, wildcard, a caller-selected budget or a rules-floor permission bypass.

The legal adapter constructs the complete destination `square_set` and its registered caption
from the same selected evidence. Its caption operands retain all move rows, and its equivalent
accessible list names every destination, promotion choice and castling choice; a count alone
is insufficient. Render human SAN/from-to/promotion labels mechanically from the retained FEN
and exact move identity; do not expose raw UCI strings in learner prose. The canonical UCI remains
in the parsed operand for exact controller correspondence, distinct from the displayed landing.
Neutral legality marks do not encode good/bad/safe/risky or human popularity.
Do not convert this set into recommendation arrows. The existing controller owns move submission,
promotion and castling choice; a hint never submits or auto-selects a move. The input dots remain
owned by `rules_floor`: coalesce identical visible destinations without a second board layer and
without replacing the controller's complete set. A discrepancy fails rather than hiding moves.

Use the pair envelope in both pre-adapter and post-adapter backstops. The budget receipt retains
the applied envelope identity/limits, actual counts and kept/dropped evidence; the strict parser
recomputes which envelope applies from the exact consumer/projection, never from a supplied flag.
Only a sole complete legal bundle may use this envelope; mixtures and different versions refuse.
The 64-mark bound comes from the board size. The 96-word bound is a proposed complete-list
envelope checked on the model's queen/promotion/castling examples, not a universal proof;
the implementation must test its actual longest rendered list rather than trust the number.
If the declared envelope cannot contain that whole set, return a typed budget-limited
answer and reopen the envelope—never take the first six moves or silently increase the limit.

For other families, retain whole atomic bundles under the existing bounded budgets. Do not
truncate a controller/contact population and call it complete. If evidence exists but its complete
bundle cannot fit, state that this view's display limit withheld it; do not reuse “No rung-0
observation is scoped to that square.” Offer Inspector only when the current role/help permits
it. No-witness, unavailable, budget-withheld and permission-withheld have separate explanations.
The request-local view chooser must not become an unfiltered fallback around this rule.

**Forms and permission.** Every selected successor has an explicit answer-content image no wider
than its parent; the legal set remains `fact | candidate_moves`, never a best move or evaluation.
Each registered renderer/parser consumes only its declared selected payload. Visible board forms
and the semantic board obey effective assistance/form limits; hidden legal destinations must not
remain in square labels or announcements. A deliberately permitted list can expose only the
same admitted set, with full identities. The complete-legality budget is not permission to render
disabled board forms, activate `showDests`, expose an opponent turn clone or bypass Match roles.

**Implementation acceptance (all still open):**

1. Genuine original source → sole selected factory/route → actual authenticated query → exact
   client parser → mounted Sight, not only a declaration/adapter census. Test all four families,
   auto and every explicit view, plus retained Patterns. No unrelated populations or source reads.
2. Complete 27-destination queen, immobile piece, empty/opponent selections, check evasion,
   en-passant, four promotions, standard castling and degenerate/non-a/h Chess960. Compare full
   lists to the original authority; prove actual controller promotion/castling submissions.
3. Empty and occupied control targets, pinned pseudolegal controller versus legal set,
   invalid-turn-clone preservation, both contact directions, locks, passer without contacts and
   both king-rights-false/rook-inapplicable outcomes. No safety, quality or strategic inference.
4. Source payloads/receipts and position-wide Inspector bytes remain unchanged. Forged/copied,
   wrong-source/FEN/clock/selection inputs fail. Genuine derived objects retain literal ancestry;
   no registered factory may accept a caller's selected output, text or arbitrary input list.
5. Old/current envelope checks, wrong version/consumer/mixture, full rendered move-list word
   counts, atomic overflow and truthful budget-limited display. Other Sight pairs still overflow
   at their existing limits. Receipt tampering and widened limits fail the real client parser.
6. Real native pointer, touch and keyboard select/request/view-change journeys on desktop and
   phone, including unrelated squares, changed position/help/role and departed delayed responses.
   Confirm no stale marks/prose, extra moves, board shrink/overflow or semantic assistance bypass.
7. Update the operation/adapter closure and normal permanent software/browser gates. Keep this
   disposable model opt-in; its green result does not discharge any item above or mobility D1.

#### 4.3 `blunder_prevention` — **no precursor; nothing to retire**

There is no staged-move warning anywhere. `Chessboard.svelte:165-185` commits straight from
`moved()` through `apply()` to `onMove`, with no interception point; the only `confirm` in the
drill path is `Timeline.svelte:83-99`'s rewind preview, which is navigation. The module needs a
**staged-move state** before commit, which the board input controller already has
(`board-input.ts:13`: `idle | origin_selected | awaiting_promotion`) but never exposes.
This is new work, and it is the only module of the eleven whose *seat* is also new.

#### 4.4 `threat_radar` — **no learner precursor; the computation exists and is fenced off**

`tactics.ts:996 threats(fen)` ships with a declared convention. Its only production caller is
`apps/server/src/candidate-evidence.ts:131`, inside `childReadings` → `candidateFeatureVector`,
which **no production code calls** — only tests and two `tools/` harnesses. `apps/web/src` contains
zero occurrences of the word. So the module is new surface over shipped arithmetic, and the
projection's `inspector_only` disposition (§2.4) is the fence being removed.

#### 4.5 `postcommit_nudge` — **precursor is replaced; three of its four behaviours are kept explicitly**

The shipped post-commit guard (`DrillScreen.svelte:964-976`, server `guard.ts`) is the closest
thing in the product to a module today, and it fires **only** under
`feedbackPolicy === "immediate_guard"` inside a pack's declared guard window
(`guard.ts:143,163,168`). The module supersedes it and must carry what it does:

| shipped behaviour | disposition |
|---|---|
| fixed headline *"The consequence exposed something concrete."* + closing *"Your played line stays preserved."* (`:967,969`) | **kept** as the module's card frame; the sentences between them become selected, budgeted packets instead of one `<p>` per evidence ref |
| **no cap** on rendered grounds | **replaced** by `maxFacts: 2` — the scarce proactive right |
| two-button dismissal: *Play on* (session-only, per event seq) and *Rewind* to the grandparent node (`:264-269,972-973`) | **kept**, as the seat's chrome; `learnerAction` stays the single contracted action (*branch from this move*), and dismissal is chrome per §1.3 |
| timeline `G` chip, `aria-label="Post-commit guard recorded"` (`Timeline.svelte:74-76`) | **kept**, and generalised: it becomes the seat's count badge on the strip |
| ambient dot tooltip flipping to *"A consequence is ready"* (`DrillScreen.svelte:845`) | **kept** |
| the `immediate_guard`-only firing condition | **widened by the module's own timing** — `post_commit`, gated by `feedbackDeliveryOpen`, in seven workflow contexts. This is a genuine behaviour change and it is the module's purpose |
| the sibling `.evidence-reveal` block and its two sentences (`:957-963`) | **kept**, as region-4 chrome, not a module |

#### 4.5a D3715 — a complete state is not a post-move change witness

**Author: Codex, 2026-10-12; refreshed and independently accepted 2026-11-04 after return.**
Acceptance covers this bounded current/ancestor selection contract, not implementation, grade
prominence, empty-result recovery or parent completion. The return and actual current-symbol
audit are recorded in `planning/learner-modules/compiler2-independent-review-2026-11-02.md`.
Local implementation and final414-case source/service/REST/mounted controls are independently
reviewed on 2026-11-04. Full13484software/all494files, isolated7/four performance, types/build,
4559authority/375semantic and governance pass. Real content stays red60 cases/two setup suites
at D3928; later corpus recipes unreached. This is not parent/native/content
or D4060 exact event-query clearance.
This amends only `postcommit_nudge` at `post_commit`, not the source
projection, generic module witness policy or the interpretation of a saved event.

The legal d2–f3 edge from
`7k/pppppppp/PPPPPPPP/8/8/8/3N4/K7 w - - 0 1` retains two
`rules.structural.event.pawn_islands@1` states, both `before: 1, after: 1`.
`createRulesStructuralEventPawnIslandsV1Evidence` correctly emits both colours, including
zero; `postcommitEdgeEvidence` currently excludes only unchanged king-zone states.
`queryModules`' generic `witnessedEvidence` does not exclude unchanged island counts.
The module can therefore spend its two proactive facts on what did **not** change.
The existing legal-edge controls in `module-query.test.ts` retain this distinction without
requiring the erroneous populated Nudge to survive.

**Proposed selection rule:** after exact source acquisition and before module admission,
novelty, reducers, ordering and budget fitting, the sole canonical query applies the same
module-specific predicate at current-subject assembly and at each earlier-user-edge assembly
in `ancestorFacts`. D3851/D3862 removed the raw legacy Nudge operation: do not restore it to
satisfy old acceptance wording. Apply the predicate to sealed, accepted evidence:

| exact source shape | required witness |
|---|---|
| `rules.structural.event.pawn_islands@1` | the named colour's `before !== after` count |
| currently accepted `rules.structural.event.<family>@1` with `StructuralSemanticEventOperands` | its complete typed `before` observation differs from its complete typed `after` observation, including appearance/disappearance (`null`) |
| `rules.king.event.zone_state@1`, if acquired and admitted | the existing king relocation or non-empty attacker/defender/shelter/escape delta; no change to `hasKingZoneWitness`'s meaning |
| other currently accepted evidence | existing source, witness and admission rules; this amendment adds no new accepts or ranking |

The structural row ranges over this module's **current exact accepts**, intersected with
`STRUCTURAL_EVENT_PROJECTION_IDS`, not a prefix match admitting future families or versions.
At this author image the exact eight structural @1 families are `backward_pawn`, `doubled_pawn`,
`half_open_file`, `isolated_pawn`, `king_opposition`, `king_zone`, `open_file` and `passed_pawn`.
Pin set equality between this finite image and the current exact acceptance intersection;
`piece_count`, `direct_attack_count` and `line_blockers` are not Nudge accepts. The separately
shaped numeric `pawn_islands@1` and detailed `rules.king.event.zone_state@1` are not cast to
StructuralSemanticEventOperands. Other sources, including grade@2 and all nine recorded-window
families, keep their existing source/witness rules and D4058 ordering. No grade priority is added.
Full observation equality ignores the enclosing FEN/move identity: a legal move changes that
identity even when the observed property stays unchanged. Compare typed values, not object
references, JSON field insertion order or a new source-hash mechanism. `sign === "preserved"`
is insufficient: `structuralEventSign` uses magnitude and can classify different observations
with the same magnitude as preserved. Existing king-zone handling is subsumed, not bypassed.
Retained observation arrays already have deterministic source order; compare every typed field,
including optional fields and nested values, not an unordered partial identity or a magnitude.

`postcommitEdgeEvidence` is shared with Review's `collectRelations`, so the new Nudge-only rule
must not be inserted into that shared source helper or generic `witnessedEvidence`. Keep complete
source acquisition unchanged and select only inside Nudge's current/ancestor admission assembly.
The server's existing endpoint-local `postcommitAt` context still supplies each ancestor's own
authorization, grade and original windows; never reuse the current tip for earlier edges. The
existing novelty window and admission/reducer ordering otherwise remain unchanged. Unavailable
sources remain unavailable, not an empty success or permission to backfill another fact.

This is **change selection, not importance or chess valence**. A changed count does not mean
good/bad, useful/harmful, forced or the engine's reason. Unchanged island counts do not imply
unchanged pawn structure; other identity-bearing changes remain eligible under their own
projection. An unchanged fact can still appear on request in Inspector or support a pack
predicate. Do not alter factories, collector populations, event signs, persisted operands,
pack schemas or semantic qualification to manufacture silence. D3687/D3690's complete-state
validation conflict and D1164's cross-node novelty are separate obligations.

**Implementation and acceptance controls (owed after independent acceptance):**

1. Reuse all legal edges in the four existing locked-pawn/pawn-free, White/Black cohorts.
   Retain the original two-colour factory population while excluding unchanged counts from
   Nudge. Do not assert every legal move is silent: a king move or another real change may
   legitimately qualify. The specific locked-pawn d2–f3 control must lose its unchanged
   island facts in the real canonical query/service operation, but must not be required to become
   empty: its actual source closure includes a lost d2 knight contribution to the White
   king zone. Preserve that changed relation. This draft does not specify selection for
   the closure's retained `rules.tactic.event.loose_piece@1` facts.
2. Add genuine gained/lost structural and changed-island-count controls. Equal-magnitude
   but different retained observations must survive; a changed FEN alone must not qualify.
   Run predecessor code against the no-change controls to demonstrate the defect.
3. Exercise current-subject and ancestor/novelty acquisition in the one canonical Nudge query,
   including the actual service and authenticated REST delivery. Exclude unchanged
   states before they consume admission or budgets; do not backfill a short/empty packet.
   Existing disclosures, exact-subject admission, ceilings and source-unavailable handling
   remain unchanged.
4. Through actual Review delivery and the original factories, preserve exact complete unchanged
   structural/island event populations and identities, despite sharing postcommitEdgeEvidence
   with Nudge. Separately, Inspector's real source query retains its complete original
   pawn-connectivity position reading, both colors/zero counts and original member identities;
   the existing position-structure and move-transition reading consumers remain unchanged.
   A corresponding reading is not an event receipt. **Bounded D4060 clarification, independently
   accepted in the compiler2 receipt, 2026-11-04:** the closed current Inspector query has no topics for
   those exact structural/island events. Their event-query coverage remains explicitly owned by
   D4060/Full Inspector source-contract reconciliation, not discharged by this reading test or
   waived from1.0. Do not add accepts/topics or restore a legacy query under this Nudge filter.
   Packs and saved event bytes remain unchanged. Neither the disposable model
   nor a passing Nudge test qualifies a semantic root.
5. Discharge D3713 separately, once a genuinely empty legal control is identified, with
   an actual API-returned empty packet: selected-card
   retirement, tablet queue head and focus after Retry disappears. Check desktop/phone and
   the populated and unavailable controls too; hiding every Nudge must fail positive controls.

Disposable source-bound checks are in
`tools/d2120-module-registration-author-contract/postcommit-change-witness.test.ts`, run by

claim UI recovery. Independent review of observation equality, current/ancestor admission,

contract on 2026-11-04 in the compiler2 receipt. Actual implementation/control review remains owed.

#### 4.5b Draft D3859 — recorded windows for the actual committed learner subject

Unaccepted amendment. The nine sequence families in the current Nudge acceptance image need
actual recorded windows, not narrative move arrays or the one-edge supplier. This explicitly
amends §2.5.2's post-commit collection boundary to include **past** granted moves ending at the
same committed learner node. It does not move the subject to the automatic opponent reply,
widen post-commit timing, predict a future reply or introduce another Nudge mode.

The service derives one private source context after current module preflight/assistance/access
admission. Its subject is the actual mutation result's user node and incoming edge. Use the
already granted selected path (including inherited nodes), not automatically the node's creating
branch or today's active tip. Validate the complete original graph/path/FENs before narrowing.
Keep original run/head, granted branch, subject node and actual commit event independently;
current role/session/disclosure and cancellation remain revalidated before publication.
No client FEN, branch/window length, narrative moves, partial synthetic DrillRun or Inspector
permission can supply this context. An opponent/system/root node is not this learner subject.

The exact sequence source image is:

| Current projection | Complete trailing horizons (plies) |
|---|---|
| derived.exchange.trade_completed@2 | 2 |
| derived.pawn.sequence.harassment_pressure@3 | 2 |
| derived.tactic.deflection_observed@3 | 3 |
| derived.tactic.attraction_observed@3 | 3, 5 |
| derived.tactic.line_blocker_clearance_observed@3 | 3 |
| derived.tactic.square_clearance_observed@3 | 3 |
| derived.tactic.interference_observed@3 | 3 |
| derived.tactic.check_zwischenzug_observed@3 | 4 |
| derived.tactic.overload_exploitation_observed@3 | 3 |

This table is set-equal to the current Nudge image's recorded sequence pairs, not every family
the generic evaluator knows. Contact timing and defender consequence remain available at their
other real consumers; this amendment adds neither to Nudge. Invoke the §4.11a proposed exact
recordedSemanticWindowSource against the retained original source, requested exact ref and
throughNodeId equal to the learner subject. Generalize its admitted server context to the actual
Nudge operation without borrowing Inspector's permission. F2 §2.3j's genuine owned @3 windows
and unchanged trade@2 inputs remain required; an old@2 result is only prototype evidence.

Evaluate only complete rows ending at that subject. The earlier opponent moves inside a window
are actual recorded play, not a candidate variation. Prepare the union of required trailing
edges at most once for each canonical source/edge; no per-family whole-prefix recomputation.
Each endpoint needs at most five prepared edges, though full original validation is not limited
to five. Never collect later stored moves then filter output. A future completed motif cannot
be shown as an already observed consequence at an earlier commit. A completed motif whose last
move belongs to the opponent is not retargeted to the preceding learner move. Review/Inspector
can expose it under their own scope/timing; changing automatic Nudge timing requires separate
intent/contract work, not a source-layer loophole.

Merge genuine sequence events with current admitted one-edge events and source-owned grade@2
before existing witness/admission/reducers/budget fitting. Retain each sequence's exact start/end
anchors, all operands and convention. No projection-id sentence fallback. Use registered exact
presentation pairs for module.postcommit_nudge@2. Source eligibility is not usefulness: the
two-fact/50-word ceiling, current ordering, no-praise rule and D3715's separately owned
change-selection policy remain distinct. A withheld/absent grade cannot hide structural facts.

Novelty retains the existing last-three-earlier-user-decision bound, not three arbitrary plies.
Resolve each ancestor's own past prefix/edge/windows under that same grant and actual ancestor
commit; never reuse today's subject or tip for every ancestor. Use admission-only ancestor facts,
the same exact source image and existing D1164 identity semantics. Share actual overlapping
preparation, not admitted facts from a different endpoint. No new novelty ranking or workaround
for its current truthful abstention. Retire the obsolete duplicate narrative/API pipeline after
its actual consumers migrate under D3851; keep one canonical module query and presentation path.

Map window_missing to the sequence binding's unavailable/no_recorded_window state; a completed
nonmatching row is no_witness, not insufficient context. Retain available rows alongside a
missing longer horizon. Source-contract/lookup failures and genuine version holds keep §4.11a's
typed failure/held-dependency channels, never silent chess absence. Missing sequence rows do not
manufacture a proactive card, all-clear or higher-priority fact; unrelated available facts remain
eligible. Request/currentness failures refuse before detector work. The client retires replies
when the exact subject/head/configuration/disclosure owner changes, without erasing saved play.

Acceptance needs genuine actor-labelled user/opponent paths for both learner colours, every
family/horizon, insufficient versus completed-empty windows, inherited and off-path subjects,
later automatic replies, stale/revoked/cancelled requests, original invalid graphs/FENs and
zero-work refusals. Actual module/HTTP/registered-component desktop/phone tests must show complete
observed sequence operands and preserve board geometry, existing presets and retained alternatives.
The disposable window target measures36 actor-labelled runs,64 learner queries/18 witnesses,
all nine families for both learner colours and46 future-witness exclusions. The actual current
one-edge source supplies none of those sequence items. This is source feasibility, not new source
issuance, automatic-opponent integration, @3 mints or delivered Nudge coverage.

#### 4.5e D3767/D4064 — selected grade feedback with complete measurement details

**Independently accepted for bounded implementation, 2026-11-04; existing compiler2 review
receipt records joint acceptance.** The independent research gate accepted
authoring this joint amendment, not its remedy. `module-evidence-assembly.md` §Current grade
selection and pinned-report presentation loss records173 research tests and159 affected software
tests:52 current grades lose at the two-fact backstop, while20 pinned mate-grade variants exceed
50 words alone. These require coordinated selection AND representation changes. No producer,
classification, ladder, preset, budget increase, pack migration or source-qualification change.

**Ordinary flow.** A selected grade contributes one short, deterministic statement to the
existing Nudge card. A directly adjacent **How measured** disclosure opens its complete original
report; no settings, Inspector mode, new engine work or game navigation is required. The report
is measurement attribution, not an explanation of why a move is good/bad. No causal join to the
other Nudge fact, new board paint, praise, recommendation or LLM-authored sentence is implied.
Absent/below-threshold/incomparable/pending/withheld grades keep their existing distinct states;
no grade becomes an all-clear or a disabled empty details button.

**Exact selection successor.** Only the current `module.postcommit_nudge@2` uses
`production.postcommit_observed_window@2` (successor of@1). Its keys, in order, are:

1. Existing critical events, unchanged: checkmate, promotion, castled and last_of_role.
2. The same eight exact completed@3 window refs from §4.5c/POSTCOMMIT_OBSERVED_WINDOW_REFS,
   preserving the accepted overload-first ordering within that group.
3. Exact `derived.grade.move_quality@2`, when the original current source emits a grade.
4. All remaining admitted facts under the current exactness/lift/accepts-order/square/event/
   retained-operand tie rules. Existing within-group ties remain; no new scored ranking.

Implement this finite key before exactness, not by changing source exactness, accepts order,
family membership or a source-prefix match. Other consumers/policies remain unchanged. Trade@2
stays in the remaining group; contact timing/defender consequence do not become Nudge accepts.
Every severity/arm has the same grade key; no extra threshold, force-slot, age/skill/rating dial
or search-quality inference. Two critical/completed-window facts may legitimately consume both
slots. The amendment repairs crowding by simpler changes, not universal grade display.

The current source acquisition, exact learner subject/horizon, unchanged-state filter, admission,
dedupe/subsumption, actual ancestor novelty/abstention, two-fact backstop and atomic fit remain.
Do not make a below-threshold grade fire, suppress a genuine critical/window fact, relabel
novelty abstention as successful suppression or reuse an opponent/later-edge grade. All standard
proactive limits stay two facts/50 words/two marks/one arrow. Acceptance cannot be obtained by
raising those limits, exempting a grade from fact count or dropping other returned sources.

**Complete registered presentation.** Evidence-presentation§3.10c owns the new exact Nudge
renderer `play.move_quality_summary@1`, its complete current grade operands and same-item explicit
report view. Nudge uses that renderer only; Review retains `grade.move_quality@2` and full prose.
Move-quality-grades§4b preserves entire public-payload and canonical full-report byte parity.
No source field or attribution is truncated or replaced by a generic sentence. The proactive
50-word fit charges the entire registered summary; explicit report rendering is a separately
named view of that same fact, with no extra fact, source query or assistance-ceiling increase.

**Lifetime and access.** Full details exist in the DOM/semantic tree only after an explicit
press. Bind that state to the actual selected packet's disclosure/subject and active seat/region,
not only componentDigest or equal grade numbers: repeated edges may have identical grade bytes.
Retire it on subject/branch/run, observed source/access/config/preset, packet replacement,
collapse/region deactivation and unmount. Old callbacks, even reinserted controls, cannot open
the old report in a current seat. Use the existing currentness/focus-recovery ownership and
companion-region scrolling; no new persistence, global modal, realtime discovery or provider
operation. Quiet/theory-only/rated/withheld flows cannot obtain a grade/details through this UI.

**Acceptance is the complete source-to-learner journey.**

1. Retain all90 grade-alone source cells and80 actual canonical-query strata. Pin actual source
   origin, requested bound, mover, full operands and current/private parent/child/branch. New
   summaries must fit all90 without trimming operands; all full reports remain byte-equal to
   their current canonical reports. Never replace truthful no-grade cases with fixture grades.
2. Record every actual selection stage. Original e-pawn grades now reach default Nudge instead
   of two slider-ray changes; quiet/repeated positives remain. Critical and completed-window
   precedence survives, including legitimate grade exclusion when their two facts fill the cap.
   All9 acquired window families and both colors/main/inherited paths remain complete.
3. Fit real registered summary+rival bundles, preserving complete rival geometry/captions and
   the shared limits. Retain genuine oversized-rival/other-family deferral negatives; do not
   assume this bounded corpus proves every possible two-fact combination fits.
4. Exact current wire tuples reject the old full Nudge renderer, partial grade operands, caller
   prose/details, wrong convention/consumer and mixed representations. Review full rendering
   and canonical report parity remain; public JSON digests are not private source authority.
5. Actual cookie REST/strict client/mounted UI prove selected grade summary and manually opened
   full report for provider and pinned-pack sources, mate/cp arms and both colors. Include
   unavailable/incomparable/below-threshold, no live executor with valid durable evidence,
   same-looking repeated edges, old-callback and run/config/access/preset/collapse retirement.
   No provider calls, saved-run changes or move/rewind actions result from inspecting a report.
6. Pointer/touch/keyboard/AT follow the same control and visible text. Test default full-report
   absence, Enter/Space, close/focus return, wrapped long attribution, no board shrink/overlay and
   independently scrolling companion region. Run normal source/types/build/software/performance/
   governance/content/native gates; record D3928 refusal honestly if it still prevents native
   startup. Fixture proof never closes default/native/qualification/parent/release obligations.

**Resources/compatibility.** Same source/consumer@2 pair, full grade payload/convention@2, module
forms and source/answer ceilings; one new renderer id and explicit product-policy successor.
Transient current Nudge receipts cut over strictly. No persisted run/pack schema, shared version
lane, new API, generic disclosure registry or executable legacy fallback. Claims remain none.
Independent joint review accepts these companions for bounded implementation. Final bounded
implementation closeout accepts D3767/D4064/D3766 locally/uncommitted:13804software/497,
7performance/types/build/source/governance pass, with complete source/selected-HTTP/UI/lifetime/
voice controls. Native rebuild succeeds then actual startup refusalD3928 prevents all journeys.
That outstanding native/default/qualification/parent/release scope is retained; D3765/D879 authenticated9-cell consumer parity now closes locally on independent review and
current200/seven+clean types; whole-parent obligations are separate. No whole-parent clearance follows.

#### 4.10a D3927 — objective transitions on one recorded Review line

**State: accepted for bounded implementation, 2026-11-02.** Separate independent review finds no
blocking specification defect; receipt and required implementation falsifiers:
`planning/learner-modules/objective-source-independent-review-2026-11-02.md`.
This acceptance does not claim implementation or parent completion. This source correction does not reopen the
grading ladder, campaign objectives or a permission ceiling. Current code establishes the gap:
`createRunRecordObjectiveTransitionV1Evidence` in `evidence-factories.ts` has only the
`{run,comparison,branchId}` arm; `comparisonFacts` checks real compared branches and
`compareBranches` refuses fewer than two distinct attempts. Its `objectiveTimeline` preserves
original `objective.state_changed` events at on-path nodes, using a fork-relative offset.
`RecordedReviewBoundary` already retains an independently validated original run, complete event
head and exact permitted path; the twelve Story adapters contain no objective adapter. These
existing sources prove that inventing a sibling/comparison is neither necessary nor valid.

**Proposed sole Review successor.** Add `run.record.objective_transition@2`, source-receipt
factory `createRunRecordObjectiveTransitionV2Evidence`, with exactly one input arm
`{recordedReview: RecordedReviewBoundary}`. Reuse the existing genuine storage-boundary reader;
no caller run, event list, outcome, FEN, from/to override or verified flag is accepted. Only the
actual current Review workflow supplies its own boundary after complete consumer/access
preflight, and rechecks its issuer, stored run, selected branch disclosure and cancellation
before publication. This factory neither grants access nor borrows Inspector permission.

Enumerate the **original full event order**, retaining every objective.state_changed event whose
data.nodeId is on that boundary's granted path. The boundary's existing whole-run replay and
path validation precede filtering. Shared-prefix events are included regardless of the node's
creating branch; sibling/off-path events and nodes beyond the granted endpoint are excluded.
This is current recorded state, not chronological replay: a legitimate later-recorded evidence
update to an on-path node remains an original event, not an excuse to crop current history.
Do not synthesize a transition from a node's final objectiveState, an imported result, engine
score or two consecutive node snapshots. Preserve repeated same-node and same-from/to
occurrences in their original order; neither an event set nor newest-only selection is valid.

Each output item has exactly `{evidence,nodeId,plyOffset}`. nodeId is the event's actual original
node; plyOffset is that node's original root-relative ply, not an invented comparison offset.
The sealed evidence payload is exactly `{context:"review",from,to}`, using the six existing
ObjectiveState values in types.ts. Its receipt binds the genuine complete boundary and original
event sequence/ordinal, including the actual retained evidenceRefs in that event's source image.
Keep those refs as original provenance, not a new assertion that an old objective was correctly
assessed. There is no evaluation, suggested move or new objective judgement in this payload.

The immutable result population, including a successful empty array, must retain authentic
factory-invocation membership against that exact boundary. Runtime Review verifies original
invocation/route/boundary and exact event lineage before admitting items; copied/reordered/
foreign or caller-empty arrays fail. A valid line with no such recorded events is available
with zero items, not a missing source. An invalid/stale boundary or failed source is never
relabelled as empty success. A genuine root event stays at ply0 in the retained source image;
Review's move rows still exclude the root. Do not attach it to the first move, invent a root
move or discard its source occurrence while claiming source completeness.

**Integration boundary.** Replace only Review@2's objective-transition@1 acceptance/binding
with@2 in the still-unfinished coordinated current image: its complete count stays72. Compare's
genuinely different comparison source remains@1 for its actual current consumer; it is not a
compatibility fallback for Review. The new exact Review pair uses a strict current-payload
parser and registered renderer over the existing OBJECTIVE_STATE_LABELS/from-to sentence,
never raw enum prose or a caller-authored sentence. Reuse the existing source-currentness and
presentation mechanisms; add no protocol, stored field, schema lane, provider task or cache.
This is a Review source alongside the exact twelve Story adapters, not a thirteenth Story
moment kind, a new ranking rule or an amendment to Story's selection. Independent ledger
acquisition remains a separate obligation and cannot substitute for this source.

**Acceptance before implementation.** Independently review this exact source contract jointly
with value-authority/Review compiler owners. Require genuine stored single-branch positive and
empty cases; repeated transitions and original event order; root and inherited-prefix events;
later valid on-prefix updates versus sibling/future-node exclusion; both learner colours;
malformed whole graph/replay, forged/cross-issuer boundary, copied/foreign/reordered output and
forged empty populations. Exercise stale/revoked/rated/cancelled workflow refusals before work
and after collection, exact registered component/HTTP delivery and unchanged Compare behavior.
No fixture may manufacture a second branch or partial DrillRun. At acceptance the producer had no
such arm. The subsequent bounded local implementation adds the sole factory and runtime collection
to the existing source owner, exact Review-only binding and registered renderer, without changing
this contract. Its source/SQLite/access/REST/component controls live in
`recorded-objective-transition.test.ts`, `recorded-review-delivery.test.ts` and
`review-map-screen.test.ts`. D3947 fences both directions of the current objective wire pair;
D3948 retains the distinct generic renderer-admission debt. This is not parent completion,
default-application/native proof or semantic qualification; the independent receipt records scope.

#### 4.11a Draft D3792 — explicit source demand and bounded source-member detail

**State: unaccepted.** This subsection supersedes no shipped protocol or adapter. D3795
inventories 75 accepted refs, measures 40 with real local/recorded inputs and leaves 35
provider/dependency refs explicitly unmeasured. Six complete facts overflow even alone.
D3797 establishes representation feasibility over 4,184 genuine source values, not useful
selection, source execution, production presentation or semantic qualification. See
`design/research/module-evidence-assembly.md` §Bounded source-member detail.

**What changes:** one Advanced Inspector can browse the existing eight families, explicitly
read a named source topic, then inspect complete typed members of one source fact. It is not
an automatic source dashboard or a new default for Play. Ordinary modules, Nudge's one-edge
scope, source meanings, source receipts and all existing module budgets remain unchanged.
No current pack needs editing to add this presentation: authored predicates continue to
consume their complete original source values.

**Compatibility and operation ownership:** add the distinct read operation
`POST /runs/:id/inspector/query`, request/response protocol `inspector.query_page@1`, owned
by this section. Do not add fields to or reinterpret `module.query_page@1`. Old module queries,
clients, presentation receipts and source bindings remain supported. Only Full Inspector's
new client uses the new operation; an older deployment gives a visible unsupported-version
state, never a silent fallback to raw evidence or the unfiltered old packet. This adds no
pack/run/storage schema, migration, semantic convention or independently allocated shared
schema/enum resource. Any later decision to introduce such a resource owes its own claim.

The request is closed and serializable:

```ts
type InspectorRequest = {
  protocol: "inspector.query_page@1";
  assistance: RequestedAssistanceV1;
  query: {
    branchId: string;
    nodeId: string;
    decisionDigest: string;
    view:
      | { kind: "inventory"; start: number }
      | { kind: "source"; projection: InspectorSourceRef; context: InspectorSourceContext;
          start: number; collectionKey: string | null; parameters?: InspectorMateParameters }
      | { kind: "detail"; projection: InspectorSourceRef; context: InspectorSourceContext;
          sourceKey: string;
          scope: { kind: "whole" } | {
            kind: "control_target"; color: "white" | "black";
            mode: "geometric" | "legal"; target: SquareName;
          };
          start: number;
        };
  };
};

type InspectorSourceContext =
  | { kind: "position" }
  | { kind: "incoming_edge" }
  | { kind: "recorded_window" }
  | { kind: "branch"; throughNodeId: string }
  | { kind: "comparison"; otherBranchId: string; otherNodeId: string }
  | { kind: "recorded_review"; throughNodeId: string };

type InspectorMateParameters = {
  kind: "forced_mate";
  maxAttackerMoves: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
};
```

**Closed source parameters (D3836):** the two exact refs
`rules.tactic.consequence.forced_mate_after_move@1` and `@2` require `parameters` on a
source request, with exactly the fields above. All other sources, inventory and detail
requests forbid it. This conditional association is a runtime parser requirement; the
optional field in the abbreviated type above is not a substitute. The Advanced Inspector
must expose the named bounded horizon; the unit is attacker moves **including the committed
candidate**, not plies. There is no caller node cap, arbitrary options bag or silent horizon
inferred from whichever caller ran last. Ordinary Hint and Review policies are unchanged.

these parameters iff the projection is one of those two refs. Collection/source identity,
disclosure, continuation and receiver equality bind the exact parameters; changing horizon
retires the old collection, pending requests and paint. The disposable request parser proves
the grammar, not production response admission or an Inspector UI default.

`sha256:` plus 64-hex form; every `start` is a nonnegative safe integer.
No client FEN, source payload, role, permission, source status, callbacks or prose is accepted.
Unknown protocol or field refuses before source work. The closed request/context and paging
shapes are exercised by disposable `tools/d3792-inspector-demand/draft-wire.ts`; it is not a
production parser, source factory, access check or implementation authorization.

recorded branch; no guessed ancestor, sibling, unplayed future or raw operand-helper construction.
For incoming-edge and recorded-window sources, the compiler supplies only its actual emitted
facts ending at this node. That rule does not turn branch, comparison or whole-game sources
into position readings. Root/no-witness, broken boundary, provider absence and failed acquisition
stay distinct.
Compile the complete registered consumer before any lazy catalogue/provider read. Disabled,
withheld, foreign, invalid-scope and stale requests perform zero source work.
Complete means set equality between the exact consumer declaration's accepted projections and
its binding projections before execution-path compilation (accepted provider contract; D3844).
Deleting refused bindings or passing an isolated diagnostic declaration cannot satisfy this
operation's full image. That guard repairs the existing compiler, not D3841's still-missing
successor declaration, adapter/presentation image or retained-source contract.

**D3841 demanded-topic absence — unaccepted author contract:** every Inspector@2 binding declares literal
`sourceAbsence: {necessity:"required",whenNoPath:"operation_unavailable"}`. Required means that
this binding's requested topic cannot be produced without an admitted complete path; it does
not require acquiring every topic when the Inspector opens. The @2 source operation performs
complete static consumer compilation first, then selects the exact requested source's bindings
from that sealed complete result. Inventory performs no resolution/acquisition. Detail demands
only its sole original source and that detail view. An unrelated provider's reach/failure never
suppresses a local topic or causes background acquisition.

For the demanded bindings only, resolve the literal source occurrences on their compiled paths
using provider exchange §2 and the exact context/source-owner map. All operands of a selected
derivation member remain required; an optional sibling path does not erase missing operands.
A satisfied complete alternative may serve the binding; absent alternatives remain distinguishable
from invalid/crossed source receipts, which fail. A required absence makes that requested topic
unavailable with the authored source-specific reason. Successful zero witnesses is `no_witness`,
not provider absence. A broken contract fails before work; no compilation failure becomes absence.

Do not call `aggregateEvidenceConsumerSourceAvailability` with a partial result list: its existing
total-consumer contract still requires every binding. This operation instead emits the one demanded
topic's source outcome from its resolved exact bindings and the source-specific outcome map.
Undemanded topics remain `not_requested` without invented satisfaction/absence rows. Neither a
temporary narrowed manifest nor a filtered consumer is permitted. An actual source-dependent
operation failure is local to this request; it is not a global unavailable family or a permission
to substitute another occurrence, old raw packet or fresh query. Required modern-source corruption
cannot fall back to a weaker historical observation. Keep missing context, source absence, failed
source, successful empty and display-limited outcomes separate through the existing closed wire.

**D3841 source-order delivery — unaccepted author contract:** after exact source/context admission,
retain the complete immutable occurrence collection, its producer order and every original ordinal.
Do not feed it through `compileModulePacket`, including its `mode:"admit"`, because that mode
still invokes `orderAdmittedFacts`; do not run novelty, criticality, exactness, lift, usefulness,
top-N or whole-packet backstops before paging. A malformed or foreign item fails the source contract,
not an invisible selection drop. Current permission/timing/answer checks remain mandatory; none
may be bypassed to preserve an inadmissible item.

The requested source page considers at most eight consecutive original occurrences from `start`,
in source order. Each occurrence is rendered through its exact registered adapter, retaining
its entire owned component bundle, equivalent sentences, captions, marks and arrows. Fit that
complete bundle atomically against every dimension of the shared policy's remaining page budget;
count its occurrence once, not once per component. The same atomic rule applies to every complete
detail member, including a statement plus a square set. If any dimension exceeds the remaining
budget, publish none of that occurrence/member's components or equivalent sentences. Preserve
the over-budget occurrence as `deferred` with its exceeded dimensions, ordinal and supported
detail door (or the member as `detail_limited`), never a partial fact marked presented or a
no-witness result. A later occurrence can fit only in that same original order;
no ranking or reordering is introduced. The page range counts all considered occurrences,
including deferred ones, so it progresses even if every original fact is too large. A request
with changed source/decision refuses stale before continuation. Detail's complete-member fitting
and `detail_limited` behavior remain as specified below; narrowing is never source re-collection.

**Required negative/native controls for these three clauses:** jointly compile genuine @1/@2
consumer images under exactly eleven policies; remove/duplicate/cross a successor image, binding,
callable, view or renderer and require refusal. Keep a permanent legal multi-adapter positive.
Prove @1 output/ordering unchanged and @2 source order unchanged even when the same facts would
sort differently by old criticality/exactness/lift. Hold/fail Maia or Explorer while requesting a
local source and require zero unrelated calls and a successful local response; inventory must do
zero source work. Independently remove a selected derivation operand and reject the crossed path;
never fill undemanded topics with absent rows. Use a multi-component occurrence/member whose
separate components each fit but whose combined words or arrows exceed the budget, and require
zero partial output and one deferred occurrence. Real authenticated HTTP and desktop/touch-phone
receivers must traverse version mismatch, unavailable/failure/retry, all-deferred pages, continued
detail, stale decision/source and permission revocation without changing board/history/budgets.
These controls are acceptance obligations, not claims of current implementation or clearance.

Local and edge factories
receive the authoritative FEN/edge; recorded successors use `recordedSemanticPath`, not v1
payload relabelling. Saved engine/tablebase values resolve exact retained occurrences. Other
providers, authored inputs, comparisons and derived dependencies must name their actual typed
supplier and source owner. The existing 35 unmeasured refs are explicit acceptance debt:
complete their supplier/input/absence/receiver/cancellation/positive-fixture matrix before this
amendment can be accepted. Do not satisfy that obligation with permanently unavailable stubs,
invented provider output, removal from the accepted image or a renderer-only inventory. Upstream
source/version holds remain; this amendment cannot authorize their unaccepted migrations.

**D3799 supplier inventory, D3800 scope correction:** the complete 35-ref worksheet is in
`design/research/module-evidence-assembly.md` §Remaining Inspector supplier matrix. It names
each exact input arm, supplier, scope and authority, not merely a matching call-site anchor.
The opt-in contract observer executes all 35 unchanged factories: 34 emit fixture values;
opening remains unavailable. Those fixtures include synthetic provider/recording data and
therefore prove neither live source acquisition nor legal recorded-path reach. Only six of
these 35 refs are acquired by today's Inspector, all from exact-node saved packets. Keep the
other source obligations open; contract fixture success does not satisfy runtime delivery.

Source scope is part of the proposed operation's contract, not inferred from `nodeId`:

| Source scope | Authoritative context | Missing context is not |
|---|---|---|
| Position | The permitted selected node's FEN and exact retained provider occurrence | A successful empty reading |
| Incoming edge | Its actual parent, legal committed move and after-node | A root with an invented predecessor |
| Recorded window | The contiguous validated played path ending at the selected node, with its exact versioned anchors | A hypothetical or relabelled v1/v2 sequence |
| Branch | The permitted recorded branch path and the source's own witness range | A point-in-time position reading |
| Comparison | Two authorised recorded attempts and their actual `BranchComparison` | A one-attempt `no_witness` result |
| Whole game | The storage-authorised recorded Review's complete admitted moments | A fabricated list derived from the selected node, or a completion prerequisite |

Pivotal option-collapse marks the first low-option decision but needs a later recorded same-side
decision to establish persistence. Its anchor and last witness node are different. Reviewing
the already-played later witness may establish the marker; a live request before that witness
may not borrow it or claim absence from a truncated execution. Branch phase transitions retain
