preceding context. Opening requires its installed/runtime identity source; an ECO/name label
is not a substitute. Story rank requires the whole admitted moment collection. Comparison
retains both exact attempts and access checks for each.

**Closed context compatibility (D3801):** branch context is required by the four pivotal refs
and deepest-opening ref; comparison by the two Compare refs; recorded Review by Story rank.
Authored shape firing accepts position or recorded branch. Recorded successors and the ten v1
move-sequence refs require recorded-window context. Exact incoming-edge factories and the five
dependent discovered/fork/overload-conflict/forced-mate refs require incoming-edge context.
Remaining FEN, packet, page and sourcing-ledger refs require position context. The disposable
grammar derives this cover from the actual accepted image/factory arms and refuses an unclassified
source; adding a ref cannot silently default to position. Detail accepts only the six sole sources
in the table below. These are context requirements, not statements of current runtime acquisition.

The server resolves all context IDs on the authorised stored run, never caller evidence:

- Position uses exactly the selected node. Incoming edge uses its actual legal parent/commit.
  A root produces `missing_context/no_incoming_edge`, not an invented edge or no-witness.
- Recorded window uses validated emitted windows ending at the selected node, with exact source
  versions and actual anchor ranges. The client cannot choose a hypothetical path/window length.
- Branch resolves the selected branch through `throughNodeId`, requires the selected node on that
  prefix, and grants only a prefix already visible under the viewer's disclosure/assistance.
  An inaccessible later witness is withheld, never treated as proof or successful absence.
- Comparison resolves both distinct recorded branch attempts and exact endpoint nodes. The
  second endpoint must belong to its declared branch; both contexts must be authorised. Derive
  the actual `BranchComparison` from those recorded prefixes. Do not silently choose the most
  recently created sibling as the existing helper does, or accept a caller comparison payload.
- Recorded Review resolves the storage-authorised recorded line's exact admitted boundary;
  selection must belong to it. The existing Review authority admits revealed unfinished native,
  imported and retry lines, and exact submitted Campaign consequences. Outcome (`unfinished`,
  recorded result or actual terminal) does not determine source existence. Successful execution
  with no moments is an empty source, not missing context. Neither source existence nor a compiled
  Inspector effect grants Inspector delivery: the current disclosure, role, Campaign entitlement
  and module-query refusals remain. No client result tag or current Play cursor replaces this join.

**D3819 actual-source boundary (2026-10-14):** opt-in
`make inspector-review-scope-research` passes 33 controls over sixteen actual authenticated
HTTP/SQLite cases. It observes the private, sealed Story-rank source only after the existing
Review checks. Two original-import reads after a live retry succeed with an Inspector effect
but the actual module query still returns `ASSISTANCE_WITHHELD`; the submitted Campaign source
also exists without an Inspector effect. An unfinished revealed line supplies nonempty moments;
a separate successful empty source has none. Completion, source availability, compiled effect
and query delivery are therefore separate axes, not interchangeable permission tokens.

The proposed supplier must reuse the current server-owned read boundary without changing
`feedbackDeliveryOpen`, cloning a Run to force disclosure or letting a Review grant authorize
live Play. Resolve permission before collector work; the existing Review constructor acquires
semantic evidence and is not a free permission predicate. Its current operation accepts only
the whole recorded branch tip, not `throughNodeId`. Exact earlier-boundary source operations,
Inspector access composition, revalidation/cancellation and native receiver proofs remain
unaccepted D3792 obligations. The disposable observer is not that supplier or a new endpoint.

**D3824 exact recorded-Review boundary (2026-10-15) — still draft:** the opt-in
`make inspector-review-boundary-research` model reads actual SQLite native games and actual
`RunService.importGame` imports, for both learner colors, with legal mate/nonterminal paths and
retries. It validates the original complete branch before extracting the requested prefix,
captures request fields and checks the current full event head plus relevant import record.
Later graph corruption refuses even for an earlier requested endpoint. Earlier prefixes and
retries cannot borrow the original PGN result; only the original imported mainline's immutable
full tip may carry it. Actual terminal events on the granted path retain learner-relative results.
The immutability condition comes from current `#refuseImportedMainlineExtension`; it is not a
general rule that any branch tip establishes an import result boundary.

The proposed **server-private** source callable is
`createRecordedReviewBoundaryAuthority(storage).resolve({runId, branchId, throughNodeId})`, with storage
owned by RunService and parser-admitted full run/import records. Its privately issued result
retains the unchanged full source image, exact bounded path, learner side and scoped outcome;
it is not Inspector access authority. The service must first obtain the separate current read/
Inspector grant above, then bind this exact source boundary to that grant. Before source work
and after any asynchronous work, re-read current storage and revalidate both authorities.
Copied/serialized/cross-issuer results, caller outcomes, off-path endpoints and changed records
refuse before source preparation. Missing/inconsistent stored import context is refusal, not
successful absence. A successful valid prefix with no moments may still be empty.

Use a distinct private source operation/identity, not the current whole-tip
`review-recorded-prefix@1` receipt. Preserve that constructor's exact input and existing callers.
The disposable `research-recorded-review-boundary@1` is **not** a production protocol/version
allocation. The model acquires actual sole-factory `run.record.position@1` and
`run.record.edge@1` items only on the granted path, retaining exact node/parent/FEN/move operands.
It does not acquire scoped semantic, pivotal, shape, objective, consequence, engine or Story-rank
sources. Those owners still owe their exact supported bindings and complete moment computation
at this boundary, not trimming whole-tip arrays. Their input/result types, private identity,
composition with current access, cancellation and HTTP/native receivers must be authored and
independently accepted before production. No new permission or complete-source claim follows.

**Closed recorded-Review rank binding ([[D3832]], 2026-10-15), still unaccepted:**
`review-evidence-compiler.md` draft §4.5 is the sole owner of
`createRecordedReviewBoundaryAuthority`, `RecordedReviewBoundaryAuthority`, `RecordedReviewBoundary`
and `RecordedReviewMomentSource` (issued by `authority.compileMoments`), including its scoped
recorded-fact arms. The D3824 paragraph above describes its precursor model, not a second
constructor or permission mechanism. Keep source assembly and current Inspector access separate.

The earlier D3832 author image proposed a new rank@2 arm. D3841 supersedes that identity choice:
keep the existing rank@2 `{moments}` contract and callers unchanged. The proposed rank@3 source
arm is `{recordedReview: RecordedReviewMomentSource}`, **held on D3845** before its declaration,
factory or adapter may be implemented. F2 §2.3g now proposes a closed fact-only collection
reduction, with provider §1a's finite templates/captured-occurrence execution contract. Neither
is accepted or executable. F2 §2.3h separately authors the last-level@2 prerequisite, still unaccepted.
Other exact suppliers, source reader,
actual receipts/admission/availability and joint review remain required; a version alone supplies none.
An opaque source handle cannot conceal undeclared lineage, and an empty local leaf/member or
fabricated eleven witnesses cannot make this requirement pass. The eventual factory asserts that source's runtime
admission and current boundary before reading its owned unrendered moments; it does not accept
the source's visible fields, copied values, a bare boundary or caller-provided moment arrays.
The existing @2 `{moments}` arm remains forbidden as the new Inspector supplier. Reuse
`rankStoryMoments`'s ordering, but bind source-kind checks to the exact new source image, including
human-divergence@2 plus phase_change@2/option_collapse@2; their old @1 markers are not new-source
witnesses. Constructing
the full Story/title then filtering is not this operation and must advance no such call counter.

The new canonical input image is `recorded-review-moment-source@1` plus the source's actual
original run/current head, branch/endpoint, learner side, boundary outcome/import record identity,
ordered granted node/edge operands, exact adapter plan/states and complete used moment/evidence/
typed-evaluation operands. It is not the opaque handle's serialization or only the displayed
rank. Equal ranks or moment images at different endpoints remain distinct scoped inputs; actual
full-tip rank values for equal valid source observations must retain legacy ordering. Keep the
current ordering convention and existing title/private/share callers; use F2 §2.3g's rank-only
convention-grounded ordering-fact rule and retain every child's unchanged authority/receipt.
Do not infer a homogeneous child's grounding or an absent family's answers onto that operation,
or mutate rank@2's meaning. This is no new
prominence, educational-value or positive-move selection policy. Exact lineage/cardinality and
source-state handling remain D3845 acceptance prerequisites, not implementation choices.

A successfully empty moment source must still produce a singleton `{rank:[]}` ordering once
the source/input contract is representable;
do not confuse zero ranked moments with a missing source or `no_recorded_review`. Present the
new Inspector topic explicitly as the recorded order of **currently admitted moments**, not
finished analysis or a complete diagnosis. Typed adapter absence/progress is retained in the
source, not invented as facts or added as undeclared payload fields. Contract/source-version
holds remain the existing unavailable result with their actual dependency, not a permanently
empty rank. No raw scores, provider images, title text or new public packet rides this arm.

Construction, factory execution and publication use the same current recorded-context grant and
signal. The receiver compares exact source scope/head/outcome and captured current assistance;
it revalidates after asynchronous preparation, while the client retires selection/configuration/
account changes independently. The source's unfinished outcome does not classify a current
recorded line as live or grant access: reuse existing original-branch read/disclosure policy,
then this operation's Inspector effect. Source existence, outcome, access and delivery remain
four different checks.

Acceptance requires the real typed arm/invoker/canonical-input/receipt and complete registered
consumer path, the source owner's §4.5 fixtures, old-arm compatibility and actual authenticated
HTTP/desktop/phone tests. All required adapter statuses and input identities survive source
assembly; scope changes, revoked access, cancellation and late callbacks publish nothing. The
current research observes300 boundary checks,25 rank controls, four invalid node-crop last-level
items and two old-input endpoint collisions; it proves why the new source is needed, not that
this source/arm or current delivery already exists. D3792 and independent acceptance stay open.

**Supplier boundary established by D3805:** the actual SQLite-backed three-branch probe shows
that `moduleComparisonForSubject` chooses the newest sibling, `compareBranches` uses both full
tips, `recordedSemanticPath`/pivotal/Review use the full recorded branch, and the sole Review
constructor refuses an extra throughNodeId before storage access. A genuine chronological replay
to the selected move loses later current reveal and both saved alternatives. It is not a scoped
current source authority. See the dated dossier and opt-in `make inspector-supplier-scope-research`.
These are existing whole-branch contracts, not defects to bypass with a shallow/filtered DrillRun.

Before this amendment can be accepted, specify and independently review one server-owned
`resolveInspectorRecordedContext` operation (proposed name, not an existing export) plus each
source-owner's supported scoped operation. The resolver reads the actual parser-sealed stored run
and current viewer/disclosure/assistance through existing authorities. It validates the requested
original branches with the total branch-path authority **before** extracting permitted paths.
It never sorts nodes into a guessed path or hides duplicate/cyclic/missing graph material by
filtering first. The selected node and every endpoint must be on its declared actual branch path;
inherited shared-prefix nodes remain valid even though their creating node.branchId differs.
Comparison branch IDs are distinct and column order is the requested primary then peer, not
the most recent sibling. A comparison endpoint must reach that attempt's recorded fork; earlier
shared-prefix position reads remain valid but cannot invent an as-yet-unreached branch attempt.

Keep **current authority** and **granted evidence span** separate. The proposed process-local,
privately admitted context retains the unchanged full stored source image, current decision/viewer
authority, exact requested selection, ordered branch/endpoint pairs and the graph-derived bounded
node/edge paths. It is not serialized caller data, a new stored run, a new cache or permission
token. Current permission cannot come from historical replay. Every source operation may consume
only its declared granted paths/occurrences, not arbitrarily call whole-branch helpers on the full
image and filter marker anchors afterward. Option-collapse must retain its complete prior/first/
later witness span; no later witness beyond the grant is borrowed.

| Source group | Required scoped supplier obligation |
|---|---|
| Local position / incoming edge | Actual selected FEN or parent/legal commit/after-node from the validated graph; root has no incoming edge; no inferred ancestor/sibling operand |
| Recorded windows / v1 sequences | Source-owner operation over validated actual granted edges, ending at the selected node. Retain exact versioned factories/anchors and complete contiguous witness spans; never fabricate a partial DrillRun |
| Authored shapes | Actual registered compiled entries and permitted recorded path; preserve provenance/trigger inputs, no caller-authored shape or guessed entry |
| Pivotal | Endpoint-aware source-owner path operation. Preserve full witness context and exact proposed refs/source limits above |
| Deepest opening | collectRecordedOpeningHistory({source,run,branchId,throughNodeId}) then createDerivedOpeningDeepestReachedV2Evidence({history}), under installed-opening §3a/F2 §2.3k. Retain complete root-inclusive rows, genuine matched/negative point invocations and authentic empty result; no whole-tip filtering or old unavailable factory alias |
| Comparison | Source-owner computation over the two exact permitted attempts/endpoints, common recorded fork and original orientation. Every timeline, outcome, evaluation and line overlay obeys its own permitted path, not just the displayed columns |
| Recorded Review / Story | Storage-authorised source-owner subject at the exact admitted recorded boundary and its complete moment image. Do not trim a whole-game rank/moment array, reuse a whole-tip receipt for an earlier endpoint or project a later import result onto an unfinished prefix |
| Provider / sourcing records | Exact retained operation/node/path/ledger occurrences from their owners. Existing operation-keyed receipt and legacy migration holds remain; this resolver does not perform an unaccepted provider exchange or invent absent evidence |

**D3841 recorded ledger suppliers — unaccepted exact source contract:** the two retained topics
are recorded.engine.eval@1 and recorded.tablebase.result@1, not aliases for the selected-node
attachment successors. The proposed server-private callable is
`RunService.acquireInspectorLedgerSource(context,{projection,signal})`. It consumes the admitted
original-run/position context from this section and exactly one of these refs, never a caller
pack id, FEN, ledger/index, source-state Boolean or reading payload. Validate requested ref/current
context before source work. Non-pack runs or missing pinned pack/sidecars yield source_absent;
they do not borrow an installed pack with the same title/id or acquire a fresh provider report.

**Registry-owned measurement admission, not grading.** Add the private registry operation
`PackRegistry.resolveRecordedMeasurementSource(packDigest)`. At actual document/sidecar
registration retain independently immutable original validated document, parsed manifest/ledger,
their existing canonical identities, original record/abstention ordinals and source links.
This source is issued/read only by the registry that owns those bytes; a caller cannot compile
one from arbitrary JSON, a matching digest, plain PackRecord/map or a spread/JSON copy. Validate
the whole document/digest, ledger/manifest shapes, exact ledger packId/packVersion/packDigest and
sourceId+retrievedAt linkage under the existing licence/source rules. Retain the full records,
including reading refusals and abstentions; the lossy positionEvidence map is not its authority.
Missing sidecars are source_absent, invalid/mismatched artifacts are source_contract_invalid,
and an actual read/lookup failure is source_lookup_failed. Existing optionalJson drops every
read/parse error to undefined; that legacy result cannot attest these distinctions. The new
registration path retains, from its original sidecar read, a closed missing/loaded/parse-failed/
read-failed state for each sidecar. Derive the old optionalJson/PackRecord behavior from it
unchanged (both failures still supply undefined to old consumers); Inspector reads the retained
typed state. Do not add a second request-time file read, fail otherwise loadable packs at boot
or call malformed/unreadable sidecars absent. Programmatic fromDocuments registration validates
its supplied parsed artifacts through the same source constructor; it does not attest an actual
file read or provider transaction. No public source issuer accepts caller failure/verified flags.

Measurement admission does **not** require objective.grading.assessedBy to be engine/syzygy.
That field supports a grading claim, not the existence of separately validated measurements.
Keep assessmentGrounding, recordedEvidence, buildPositionEvidenceIndex, recordedReadingsAt and
phase composition behavior unchanged. The new admission neither verifies an authored objective
nor reclassifies an old pack. Missing/unverified old grading metadata is not, by itself, a source
version hold. The new source checks actual artifacts; it does not promote their historical
reports to modern provider exchanges or manufacture a durable receipt that old values lacked.

**Exact selected population.** Canonicalize the actual selected run.record.position@1 FEN with
the existing legal six-field FEN routine. Both engine and tablebase matching use all six fields,
including side, castling/en-passant and both clocks—not transposeKey or halfmove-only matching.
Inspect every requested-kind record/abstention in original order. An invalid requested-kind FEN
is a source-contract failure, not permission to omit it and assert absence. Where record.values
also owns a FEN it must canonical-match its anchor. An engine manifest's owned FEN and a recognized
Syzygy /standard request FEN must likewise match that exact record; conflicting claims fail.
Retain other permitted origin forms as historical source links, without inventing a provider
transaction. Full source links and raw prose remain private; none are LLM-created chess facts.

For each matching record invoke exactly sourcing.ledger.engine_eval@1 → recorded.engine.eval@1,
or sourcing.ledger.tablebase_result@1 → recorded.tablebase.result@1. Retain the original ordinal,
exact sealed ledger/value pair, actual factory outcome and source association in the owned
result. Assert exact same-record lineage, not just matching FEN/payload. An actual
ledger_record_not_admitted outcome is a retained per-record refusal, not source corruption,
empty success or a budget-deferred fact. An exception, crossed value or failed required invocation
fails the source. Keep all admitted readings in original order and multiplicity: do not choose
newest/best, deduplicate same-FEN reports or hide them beside live attachments. Matching ledger
abstentions remain separate source statuses, never negative chess evidence.

Use the existing canonical-FEN and declared-reading checks from phase composition where
applicable, but do not alias its singleton tablebase snapshot/resolver: that API rejects duplicate
full-FEN readings and supplies no engine population. Preserve it unchanged. The complete new
private result retains the pinned pack/manifest/ledger identities, selected run/head/grant/node/
position/projection, every matching record/disposition and matching abstention, including empty
lists. Its sourceKey uses the existing canonical source-identity construction over this complete
image. New compiler occurrence witnesses bind those actual factory invocations/ordinals; old
values/receipts are never retroactively stamped. No new evidence projection, schema or provider
operation is needed for this source result. Source permission and actual execution are distinct.

**Mixed and zero populations.** Add the closed recordedCoverage object below to the draft wire.
It describes artifact enumeration and measurement admission, not confidence or presentation
budget. If any readings are admitted, outcome is available with collected equal admittedRecords;
all valid readings remain pageable even beside refusals/abstentions. If no reading is admitted
but any record was refused or any matching abstention exists, outcome is
`unavailable/recorded_measurement_unavailable`, with that same complete coverage. Only a validated
source with zero matching records **and** zero matching abstentions yields no_witness. Missing,
invalid, unreadable or genuinely held source states carry null coverage, not fabricated zeros.
Source_contract_invalid/source_lookup_failed use the existing failed outcomes; genuine version
holds use the exact actual dependency rule, never pending metadata alone. Revalidate current
access/decision and the current registry-owned artifact image after any async boundary and before
publishing; a surviving node/unchanged pack document does not waive changed ledger/manifest state.
Forward cancellation and perform no provider, disk re-scrape or whole-run attachment mutation.

Before acceptance require real registry issuance/load/hydration/removal tests, forged/copied/
cross-pack/ledger/source/position/ref refusals and actual complete ordered mixed populations.
Exercise engine/tablebase clock/side mismatches, same-FEN duplicates, live coexistence, grading
independence, malformed/unverified/absent/read-failed and factory-refused sources. Prove strict
coverage/receipt correlation and native rendering of valid facts beside partial/refused/abstained
coverage without fake facts or raw JSON. Re-run old phase/lookup/pack callers unchanged. The
disposable synthetic-ledger experiment proves selection/factory feasibility only, not installed
source issuance, a real engine/tablebase computation, qualification or authenticated API/native
delivery. The complete75+6 consumer/source obligations and independent joint review remain held.

**D3836 dependent incoming-edge binding (2026-10-15), still unaccepted:** the proposed
`prepareInspectorDependentEdgeSource` supplier takes an actual admitted
`DeclaredEvidence<RecordedEdge>` from `run.record.edge@1`, one of the five exact refs below,
and the closed mate parameters only for the two mate refs. This is a proposed supplier,
not an existing export or permission granted by this draft. Its server owner obtains the
edge from `resolveInspectorRecordedContext`'s validated **original** graph and granted
selection. No client FEN, guessed parent, shallow replacement Run or later played successor
supplies its operands. Retain requested branch/endpoint separately from the edge's creating
branch: an inherited shared-prefix node remains valid on a child branch.

| Exact proposed dependent source | Required dependency/factory path |
|---|---|
| `derived.tactic.discovered_executed@1` | Before-position `rules.tactic.reading.discovered_latency@1` plus same-edge `rules.transition.event.slider_ray@1`, then the unchanged target factory and its gained-ray semantics |
| `derived.tactic.nonterminal_fork_survival@1` | Same-edge `rules.tactic.event.double_attack@1`; a successful empty event population is `no_witness`. Otherwise use its one actual event and matching same-edge `rules.tactic.consequence.reply_breadth@1`, then proposed createDerivedTacticNonterminalForkSurvivalV1Evidence under value-authority §2.3f. Terminal false is available, not no_witness/unavailable; full private edge join precedes that result. The D3836 diagnostic still exercises old derived@1 |
| `rules.tactic.predicate.overload_conflict@1` | Pass the actual selected edge's canonical beforeFen/moveUci/afterFen to proposed `createRulesTacticPredicateOverloadConflictV1Evidence`; it validates the complete legal edge before capture/quiet work and computes capture/duties/hypothetical replies internally under value-authority §2.3e. No sealed prerequisites, caller capture or result override; this is not an existing export. The D3836 diagnostic still exercises old derived@1 |
| `rules.tactic.consequence.forced_mate_after_move@1` | Same-edge `rules.tactic.consequence.reply_breadth@1`, beforeFen and explicit `maxAttackerMoves`, then the exact v1 factory |
| `rules.tactic.consequence.forced_mate_after_move@2` | The same dependency scope and explicit horizon, then the exact v2 factory; never relabel a v1 payload |

The supplier retains the actual edge, exact dependency items and genuine factory outcomes.
The nonterminal-fork row supersedes only D3836's new-Inspector unchanged-fork proposal; old source
APIs/receipts/helpers remain. The computed strict-overload row supersedes D3836's earlier unchanged-derived@1 proposal and
D3852's rejected derived@2 alternative only for the new Inspector. Its factory reconstructs all
authority from the legal edge; do not implement it by relabelling old sealed outputs or silently
consuming/discarding sealed prerequisites. All existing F2 arms, receipts, versions
and source meanings remain unchanged; those receipts
alone are not current-context authority. Special moves retain the source's actual identities:
an en-passant captured target is not its landing square. No co-defender or motif verdict is
guessed from a partial population. Strict overload's `no_legal_recapture` is **unavailable
only for that exact projection**, never clear/no_witness, a conflict, or an engine grade.

Reply breadth and mate trees are bounded **hypothetical consequences**, not later recorded
play borrowed beyond the grant, player intent, significance or an explanation of why an engine
chose the move. Preserve `proved`, `refuted` and `budget_exhausted`; exhaustion is not refutation
or absence. Preserve v1's complete operands and v2's exact nine fields: `beforeFen`, `candidate`,
`afterFen`, `attacker`, `maxAttackerMoves`, `proofStatus`, `proofDigest`, `rootReplies`, `nodes`.
The existing `mate-proof@1` convention and 250,000-node cap remain; no client override is added.
Hint's fixed two-attacker-move policy and Review's engine-reported distance-plus-one (at most
four) remain distinct existing policies, not an undocumented shared Inspector default.

Complete consumer/source/version admission and current access checks precede dependency work.
Execute only the requested dependency path, not the whole candidate population or a broad
semantic closure subsequently filtered. These current factories are synchronous and do not
promise mid-computation cancellation. Existing operation deadline/current-receiver checks
apply before and after computation; late/cancelled results cannot publish. Any execution
isolation or yielding needed to honor that deadline needs implementation proof, not a raised
budget or dropped operands. Source/version/convention/definition and qualification holds all
remain. No new F2 arm, schema claim or production authorization is implied here.

The disposable probe exercises eight legal fixtures in both colors, all four existing mate
horizons and two actual stored imports (six edges), with 318 controls/218 source requests.
It checks original-graph refusal before narrowing, inherited prefixes, pinned recaptures,
en-passant target identity, empty/unavailable separation and unchanged saved play. Its roughly
276 ms observed maximum is dependent-stage cost on this small local population, not a general
latency guarantee, API/native proof or semantic qualification. Production acceptance still
owes typed source/context/response binding, horizon-crossing/stale/cancel controls and actual
authenticated/native delivery. See `module-evidence-assembly.md` §Dependent incoming-edge sources.

**D4082 paired current-shape migration (independently accepted, 2026-11-04).** Separate
delivery and source/execution reviews accept the repaired bounded contract; decisions are in the
existing compiler2 review receipt. It depends on F2 §2.4b and does not redefine the accepted firing2
paragraph below. The foundation 23+5 source inventory is unchanged. Introduce exact current
`theory.shapes.firing@3` bindings and registered presentation tuples; preserve truthful old
declarations instead of rewriting their dependencies or accepting either version on one current
tuple. The existing declaration generator/lifecycle checks perform this coordinated cutover.

Inspector replaces its one modern shape topic with firing3 (same topic count, position/branch
contexts). Its actual registered supplier awaits the complete source-owned scan, preserving
original context/graph/current grant, complete invocation and existing unavailable/no-witness
distinction. Topic descriptors, exact Full2 pair, factory route, selected renderers, source
adapter, strict receiver and paging/detail controls migrate together. No local `{entries,path}`
fallback or direct JSON re-sealing. The Inspector confirmation/disclosure workflow is unchanged.

Ordinary guided board recognition is a separate authorized read, not a hidden Inspector call.
Add `POST /runs/:runId/shape-firings/query` to the existing authenticated JSON read-query
RunService/REST/client path, with closed `{assistance:RequestedAssistanceV1,
query:{branchId,endpointId,decisionDigest}}`. The requested browser intent is legitimate input;
it is not a claimed ceiling, finalized config or permission. Parse through existing assistance
intent and read-query/decision helpers; refuse unknown fields and caller FENs, shape lists,
trigger objects or finalized permission claims. The service validates actual run access,
graph/edges and branch endpoint, resolves the requested intent against actual server availability,
role/session/ceiling, and refuses source work unless guided is live at
that actual ceiling. Scope is the branch's complete root-to-requested-endpoint path, never its
later tip. Missing registry returns unavailable; registry/contract faults are failures, not
empty success. Existing disconnect cancellation/current-read retirement applies throughout
cooperative capture. This read writes no run/event, requests no engine/provider and grants no
additional assistance.

The closed response is `{runId,branchId,endpointId,decisionDigest,effectiveConfigDigest,sourceKey,
outcome,firings}`. Outcome is exactly `available`, `no_witness` or
`{kind:"unavailable",reason:"authored_source_absent"|"source_dependency_unavailable"}`;
the first two are represented as `{kind:"available"}` and `{kind:"no_witness"}`. Unavailable
and no-witness carry an empty firings array; available carries one or more complete items.
Each firing is `{value,presentation}`: value is the full RegisteredShapeFiring payload and
presentation is the existing registered PresentedEvidenceItem population from exact current
`theory.shape_firing@2` bound only to firing3. Its exact adapter is
`theory.shape_firing@2 × theory.shapes.firing@3 × inspector.registered_shape_firing@2 ×
shape-catalogue@1`, using the existing complete registered-shape operands and sentence/panel/
timeline-marker forms. The new Inspector firing3 tuple uses the same renderer/convention with
its actual Full2 consumer/context. Preserve existing sentence/panel/
timeline-marker forms and existing budget rules; do not turn matched catalogue plans into
validated advice. sourceKey binds the original complete source/path/invocation, not just visible
positive items. decisionDigest/effectiveConfigDigest use the existing current run/assistance
decision helpers. No private token or raw trace population is serialized to the browser.

The strict client checks the closed envelope, original requested run/branch/endpoint, canonical
firing3 consumer/presentation tuples, attribution/interval endpoints and response outcome before
the mounted seat accepts it. It also checks request-local decision/config expectations, retains
the exact received source key and retires on any current run/branch/endpoint/assistance change,
401/403, registry invalidation or later stale completion. Refresh after actual source-affecting
changes; never display a stale response while recomputing the new scope. DrillScreen replaces
local shapeFiringEvidence(shapes,path) with this delivered population and existing loading/empty/
unavailable state. Catalogue data supplies navigation and authored plan text only. Marker labels
and panel recognition attribution come from admitted values. Recognition follows the same
guided ceiling in visual and semantic views. Preserve existing focus retirement/return behavior.

The two point modules use the same source-owned preparation before their existing pure selection/
render steps: structure_nudge and theory_breadcrumb receive the original point scan over their
exact timing subject. Replace their current raw-shapes input path and exact projection lists/bindings together;
do not pass registered JSON into a legacy matcher. Migrate only shape-source members, preserving
their existing selection policies, budgets, ordering, ceilings and nonshape sources. Current
module source-list and consumer changes use the existing coordinated successor generator rather
than rewriting frozen accepts. No extra prominence or statistical chess judgement is authorized.

Story/guidance remain a separately owned **unimplemented D4077 cutover**: actual storyMomentsForRun,
guidance.voice_story, review.story and derived.story.rank@2/derived.story.title@2 still depend on
firing1; the authentic rank3 reduction's closed element list also needs a truthful successor if
fed firing3. This bounded amendment authorizes none of those dependency rewrites or new rank/title versions.
They require an exact independently reviewed downstream amendment before their migration; adding
firing3 to a top-level consumer without its real derived inputs does not close them. No remaining
live firing1/2 path may be represented as complete current foundation adoption.

Make RunService.shapeRecommendations asynchronous and await it at GET /progress/recommendations.
Capture only the actual authenticated learner-owned run population; for every branch await the
same owned complete scan, then preserve the existing per-run deduplication, countable attempted
shape exclusion, matching present-shape pack links, run-count sort/id tiebreak and ten-item limit.
Keep the existing public recommendation DTO. Capture the actual learner-owned run/head/branch
population once and recheck it and the authenticated principal at every chunk/yield and before
publication, using the existing storage/access owner. Pass the actual request AbortSignal.
Missing required registry/progress/pack configuration, unavailable branch or failed scan refuses
the **whole endpoint** through existing typed HTTP service-unavailable/source-failure errors;
never merge a successful repertoire page with a fabricated successful empty shape contribution.
An installed genuinely empty shape registry is a valid empty contribution. No stored fact/style/
skill credit, provider request or write is added.

In addition to F2's complete source falsifiers, permanent real HTTP/strict-client/mounted controls
cover both colors, positive/negative/explicit-ref truth, every disappearance/return prefix,
inherited branch and repeated FENs, silent/limited assistance before source work, changing
grant/config/run/cursor during capture and after delivery, stale/out-of-order responses, no
future endpoint, semantic assistance parity and focus lifecycle. Recommendations cover multiple
branches in one run, repeated encounters in distinct runs, actual countable-attempt exclusion,
missing/unavailable source and no writes/providers. Current module/Story operation tests prove
source membership and exact tuple migration; an anchor census alone does not prove consumption.
Complete normal software/content/governance and separate implementation review remain required;
neither this contract nor a fast primitive benchmark qualifies a source or closes a capability.

**D3972 bounded clarification, independently accepted 2026-11-02.** Exact decision and returned
bootstrap/probe-order repair: `planning/learner-modules/compiler2-independent-review-2026-11-02.md`. F2
§2.4a specifies the missing actual registered-document/trigger source owner and exact
`theory.shapes.firing@2` successor. Only the modern direct Inspector shape
topic replaces v1 with that exact source-backed successor, preserving one of65 topics and
position/branch contexts. Its authority includes actual registered document identity/version/
digest, literal `/trigger`, registered name/channel/handle and original complete invocation.
The registry issues/checks the source; runtime owns factory execution/proof. Empty installed
sources and actual missing registry remain distinct; metadata alone never establishes a hold.
The following earlier `{entries,path}` interval rules remain the algorithm/scope basis, not
authorization to treat their legacy receipt as registered provenance. Whole captured Review
uses retain their accepted weaker trigger authority until separately migrated. No new schema
lane or generic provenance framework, strategic claim, partial public consumer or self-acceptance.

**D3833 authored-shape interval binding (2026-10-15), earlier source arm:** resolve entries
through RunService's actual `ShapeRegistry`, not caller triggers, names, rendered plans or
an independently copied catalogue. Capture the selected registry records, their document
identity/digest and exact triggers independently of mutable caller arrays. Revalidate current
access/source ownership before preparation and publication; a replaced registry source retires
the result even when its display name is unchanged. The existing registered-authored-provenance
hold remains: a loaded JSON file or a successful legacy factory call cannot discharge it.

For position context, evaluate the sole selected `{id,fen}` node. For branch context, run
the existing `shapeFirings` algorithm over the complete granted root-to-endpoint path only,
after original graph/FEN validation. Use the existing `theory.shapes.firing@1` factory's
`{entries,path}` arm over that exact source, retaining its actual entry/trigger source digest
and complete bounded path input. The current resolved-context/response envelope retains
requested branch/endpoint authority separately; the legacy value receipt alone is not that
grant, a registry-provenance receipt or a new scope protocol. No new F2 arm or version is
implicitly allocated by this paragraph.

Preserve entry ordering, separate repeated intervals and inclusive first/last node semantics.
`openEnded` means the trigger still matches **at this granted path's endpoint**, not that the
pattern persists into later play. Do not crop whole-path intervals, clamp a later end id or keep
the whole-path closure flag. A singleton position does not assert the pattern began there in
the game; a successful empty trigger population does not prove no useful plan exists. Return
actual source/contract holds as unavailable, not a manufactured empty population. This binding
renders trigger matches only; it never validates the entry's strategic prose or significance.

The disposable actual-registry/SQLite control uses all 25 installed entries and legal fianchetto
disappearance/return for both learner colors. 22 prefixes retain 20 declared items; naive cropping
has four future endpoints and eight future closure claims. Full-tip parity, empty values,
copied/cross-issuer refusal, original later-FEN refusal before preparation and unchanged saved
imports are required controls, not production proof. Review compiler §4.5's shape adapter reuses
these same interval semantics. Actual registered provenance, current API/client admission and
native delivery remain open with D3792; this subsection is not independent acceptance.

**D3820 comparison source-owner boundary (2026-10-14):** opt-in
`make inspector-comparison-source-research` first proves the unchanged constructor fails the
exact requested endpoint. Its guarded constructor prototype passes 116 controls over 62
endpoint pairs/four legal both-color fixtures, including actual mate outcomes, fork-only
and unequal-length prefixes, reversed columns and original late-graph corruption refusals.
Every row, objective/checkpoint timeline, eval/line overlay and outcome stays on its own prefix;
full-tip comparison output and the original run remain unchanged. Recorded eval/checkpoint/
objective controls are explicitly synthetic, not live provider or strategic evidence.

That constructor alone is insufficient: `comparisonFacts` recomputes full `recordedBranchFacts`,
and `structureDeltaEntries` independently scans both complete branches. Passing the bounded
comparison to the unchanged structural factory returns 32 later observations. A second guarded
source-owner experiment narrows BOTH paths before the cross-column intersection. Its 246
controls retain the complete expected ordered population, 1,020 items over the same 62 pairs,
without later observations or an empty-population shortcut. The expectation uses current
structural readings to test source scope, not an independent oracle for those chess predicates.

Before acceptance, author the source-owner callable and its privately admitted two-endpoint
input/result plus every consuming factory binding. A bounded display `BranchComparison` is
not that input or a proof of current permission. In particular, record-fact recomputation,
engine verification, structural intersection and piece routes must each consume their granted
paths, not reopen whole branches or trim already-selected output. Retain existing whole-tip
operations. New input arms, operation/receipt identities and any required shared version claims
must be explicit and independently accepted; neither prototype supplies production typing,
current v1 scoped receipts, response admission or HTTP/native receiver proof. Do not reinterpret
a full-tip receipt or silently replace any of Inspector's 75 source obligations.

**Comparison source constructor and execution obligations (2026-10-15), still unaccepted:** The
current Full Inspector inventory accepts `derived.compare.structure_delta@1` and
`derived.compare.eval_delta@1`; D3841's proposed successor image replaces the latter with @2.
Structural lineage is separately proposed by D3847/F2 §§2.3d/2.3i; this subsection does not authorize
adding a scoped arm to its frozen @1. Inspector does not accept the route or trajectory projection merely
because Compare does. The new operation must invoke the requested factory directly. Calling
`comparisonStrips`, `comparisonNarrative` or `compareSources` and filtering afterward acquires
unrequested siblings and is not this operation. Existing full-tip Compare, its routes and all
its other factory arms remain supported and unchanged.

The proposed runtime source constructor is
`createRecordedComparisonSource({run, columns})`, called only after the server resolver has
granted both recorded spans. `run` is its unchanged, parser-admitted complete stored image;
`columns` is exactly a readonly two-tuple of closed `{branchId, throughNodeId}` records, in
primary/peer order. Strings are captured once, nonempty and independently retained. This
constructor establishes recorded source scope, **not storage authenticity, current access,
Inspector permission or permission to request a provider**. Those remain the resolver's
separate responsibility, including the post-acquisition check.

The source has a nominal TypeScript identity and runtime-owned admission (a module-private
WeakMap or equivalent). Its internal data is not accepted as a constructor argument, a factory
payload, JSON or wire token. The constructor and type may cross the runtime/server boundary
for the new operation; the data reader stays runtime-internal to the source/factory owner.
The complete operand domain, invoker and declaration census must include this exact new
arm before it can execute. A cast, spread, JSON copy or genuine source from another run/scope
cannot substitute for the requested source at the query receiver.

Constructor order is: admit the closed argument and original run, independently snapshot its
complete unchanged data and captured column tuple, reject equal/unknown branch ids, validate
**both complete original branch graphs and every original path FEN**, then locate either
endpoint and extract the two exact prefixes. Use the same legal-position admission as the
existing structural source (`positionFromFen`), not only FEN syntax or `branchPath` traversal.
A valid graph or event replay is not proof that every original position is valid; terminal
event replay validates some FENs but ordinary committed nodes can still carry invalid ones.
Validate both columns as a set before any structural source access, including cache hits,
engine lookup or evidence mint; a malformed later FEN on either column cannot be hidden by an
earlier endpoint. No structural facts are computed during this validation. Deep-freeze only
the independent source-owned snapshot, never caller objects; later caller mutations cannot
change captured nodes, events, scopes or receipts. Each prefix must contain that branch's actual recorded fork. Compute
the common fork from the two ordered prefixes, not from caller metadata, current cursor,
node.branchId, newest sibling or full-tip comparison. Inherited nodes are valid; an endpoint
before its attempt's fork is not. A valid pair ending exactly at the common fork remains valid
and may emit zero differences. Do not manufacture a shortened DrillRun or a partially populated
BranchComparison. No structural reader, engine lookup or evidence factory runs in construction.

The internal source owns only the unchanged run image, captured two-tuple, original validated
paths, granted prefixes and actual common-fork node. Its canonical operand image has the new
operation identity `recorded-comparison-source@1`, the original run id/current
`ModuleDecisionStamp`, ordered endpoint records and ordered original node/edge identities for
both granted paths. Original FEN, normalized committed move and event occurrence operands stay
attached to their source records. This is a distinct source-operation identity, not a new
persisted protocol, permission token or reinterpretation of a whole-tip value receipt.

The scoped source obligations below do not introduce additional @1 arms. Eval dispatch must use
F2 §2.3a's exact successor and sole arm; structural dispatch uses the separately proposed
§2.3i source/factory/reduction contract, still held before acceptance:

| Source requirement | Arm status | Source-side execution and literal inputs |
|---|---|---|
| `derived.compare.structure_delta@2` | F2 §§2.3d/2.3i: collectComparisonStructuralPopulation({comparison}) then sole `{population,branchId}` factory arm, not a new @1 arm | Require branchId equal one granted column; both fork-inclusive paths retain actual positions and17 ordered exact-family results, including authenticated successful empties. Preserve complete peer unions, predecessor absence/reappearance and relative offsets; no positive-only or whole-tip substitute. Authenticate the complete result array before paging/flattening and join each item to its original ordinal/location. Named catalogue identity is explicit new @2 payload; registered full-detail renderer preserves pawn reach. Grammar/source/renderer/joint acceptance and actual runtime remain held. |
| `derived.compare.eval_delta@2` | `{before,after,comparison: RecordedComparisonSource,branchId:string}` exactly, per F2 §2.3a | Source owner reconstructs the complete recorded trail on the granted column and selects consecutive report pairs in stable ply/event order. Both exact sealed recorded.inspector.eval_packet@1 inputs, event/head/node membership and the comparison source must validate; arbitrary supplied pairs are not a trail. Preserve safe-integer cp parsing and STORY_PIVOT_CP, with no mate conversion. Emit the new recorded_cp_numbers/provider_not_established frame and disclose unverified comparability; do not label this a modern validated engine delta. |

The old `{run, comparison, branchId}` arms remain unchanged for their existing callers.
Do not infer scoped admission from `comparison.columns[].leafNodeId`, add an optional endpoint
to that old arm, overload the old receipt or accept caller arrays of observations/trails.
The new factories admit the runtime-owned source before reading its data and mint from its
canonical operand image **and actual used records**, never from the opaque handle's empty
serialization or only the run/branch/fork. Different endpoint tuples must have different input
identities even when their displayed deltas happen to be equal. Reversing the tuple retains
requested column order and changes scope identity without manufacturing another chess meaning.

The structural successor must preserve the current appearance/noncommon selection and legacy
observation/location population at complete tips, while supplying truthful complete source lineage
and its exact grounding/convention contract. Its new namedStructure field is the explicit additive
payload change in F2 §2.3i, not whole-payload byte equivalence. This proposed schema is not implicitly
accepted by the source constructor. Eval@2 deliberately reports recorded numerical difference under F2 §2.3a's weaker
recorded_run authority and explicit comparability disclosure; it is not byte-equivalent to the
old provider-labelled @1 projection. Existing @1 factories, full-tip callers and historical
receipts remain untouched. Independently review each successor, its actual factory/consumer
bindings and compatibility; no silent predecessor reinterpretation or retirement.

In particular, an `engine_validated` attachment label does not establish a modern provider
receipt. The old eval@1 factory keeps its provider-exchange dependency and pending status;
the proposed recorded @2 is a separately requested weaker view, not a fallback from a modern
source refusal. It cannot manufacture engine/model/bound identity or discharge that upstream
source migration. Where the requested consumer requires a held source version, return the
existing `unavailable/source_version_held` with that actual dependency—not a universal stub,
successful absence or a new stronger claim. A permitted complete execution with no qualifying
delta is `no_witness`; invalid attachment/source operands are `failed/source_contract_invalid`.
Invalid scopes are the existing request refusal. Missing the second attempt is resolved before
construction as `missing_context/no_second_attempt`, not an empty fabricated comparison.

Acquisition consumes this source synchronously and requests neither provider nor unrelated
projection. Observe cancellation before source construction, before source/factory work and
before publication; asynchronous outer preparation still uses the resolver's same signal and
post-await storage/access/configuration revalidation. The query receiver compares the admitted
source's captured run, both branch/endpoints and current decision with the resolver's exact
grant before its values can become source summaries/detail. The browser independently retires
old request owners, scope/configuration/account changes and paint. A correct source object is
not permission to publish an obsolete response.

Acceptance adds actual typed constructor/successor/invoker controls to the D3820 prefix
population. The eventual accepted structural successor preserves full-tip selection and legacy
observation/location bytes, with its explicitly new catalogue-identity field;
eval@2 preserves the specified historical pair selection/arithmetic with its explicitly new
payload, lineage and comparability disclosure. All old@1 callers, payloads and receipts remain
unchanged. Fork-only/unequal-length/reversed and inherited prefixes retain exact complete
source-scoped output; original late-graph corruption and malformed
or syntactically valid-but-illegal later FENs on either original column refuse before any read
counters advance, including warmed memo accesses. Controls replay actual committed events and
distinguish parser-rejected terminal images from replay-admitted ordinary images; neither a
parser exception nor a warmed detector cache can falsely satisfy the source-boundary negative.
After construction, mutating each caller-owned node/event/column input leaves the independent
source and output unchanged without freezing the caller. No source from another endpoint/order/run, copied handle or substituted
branch is accepted. Equal displayed payloads at different endpoint tuples must still retain
distinct declared input scopes. Later structural observations, eval attachments and terminal
results cannot enter an earlier grant. Preserve complete source operands, original saved bytes,
mate boundaries and current legacy dependency status. Actual authenticated HTTP/client/desktop/
phone tests must exercise both requested refs and stale/cancelled/revoked results through the
new route. The existing guarded algorithms do not already prove these typing, identity,
permission or receiver criteria; all remain unaccepted implementation work.

Supplier rows not specified below still owe their exact callable/input/absence/receiver/
cancellation and boundary fixtures. Named operations are requirements, not claims of landed code.

**D3825 pivotal source-owner boundary (2026-10-15), still draft:** the proposed private
`pivotalPayloadsForRecordedContext(context, {projection})` consumes only the resolver's owned
original-run/granted-path context and one of the four literal pivotal refs. It validates current
context before work, computes on the granted path before selection, and returns privately owned
payload/witness records, not public v1 evidence or an access token. Irreversibility needs the
actual parent/commit; phase change needs the previous definite phase (possibly across unclear
nodes) and current node; sustained option collapse needs prior/first/later same-side positions.
Its display anchor is the first low-count node, not its witness endpoint. A prefix containing
that anchor but not the later witness cannot receive the marker. Do not filter a whole-tip
marker array by nodeId. Existing whole-tip calls stay unchanged.

Opt-in payload-owner research passes 465 controls across 207 prefixes of seven actual stored
legal runs. Two collapse paths come from the existing CC0 fixture; distribution controls are
synthetic metadata committed through the real runtime, not live Maia measurements. Four
later-witness exclusions, original late-graph refusals, shared-prefix/foreign-endpoint controls
and full-tip/saved-byte retention pass. This is payload/scope research, not scoped factory,
permission, semantic qualification or native delivery proof.

**D3826 exposes a source-binding defect:** an actual rewind/fork with two
labelled distribution controls emits both selections at a shared node on either branch. The
current pivotal factory binds the latest node-level human-policy source to both; one marker
per branch therefore disagrees with its declared engine source. The accepted-contract repair
retains each marker's actual recorded engine/candidate image and uses the existing closed
human-policy page arm, preserving current whole-tip populations and public input signatures.
Its permanent regression first fails; repaired actual stored controls have zero crossed inputs.
That repair is not a scoped source authority. An exact recorded-selection occurrence must bind
the proposed scoped marker and source together; a latest-at-node lookup cannot do it.
Before acceptance, specify the closed source/factory occurrence input, original branch/edge
membership, exact event sequence, scoped receipt identity and legacy compatibility. Require
both-source/both-branch refusals and inherited-prefix controls, not a new page manufactured
from marker fields. The 75-ref source obligations and independent review remain; a scoped
payload list alone neither resolves this binding nor becomes a current v1 receipt.
New source-owner signatures/receipts or projection meanings require explicit accepted contracts
and version declarations; this amendment does not silently reinterpret v1 whole-branch suppliers
or retire any of the 75 source obligations. Existing full-tip calls remain supported.

**Closed pivotal source and factory contract ([[D3831]], 2026-10-15), still unaccepted:**
The following replaces the proposed `pivotalPayloadsForRecordedContext` signature above; that
name is not a second supplier to implement. Introduce exactly one runtime-owned scoped source:

```ts
// Proposed exports, not existing production symbols. The brand/admission reader is private.
declare const recordedPivotalSourceBrand: unique symbol;
export interface RecordedPivotalSource {
  readonly [recordedPivotalSourceBrand]: true;
}
export function createRecordedPivotalSource(input: Readonly<{
  run: DrillRun;
  branchId: string;
  throughNodeId: string;
  kind: PivotalKind;
}>): RecordedPivotalSource;
```

The owner is `packages/runtime/src/pivotal.ts`; export only the constructor/nominal type through
the existing runtime entry point for the server operation. Retain one private path-reading
implementation shared with whole-tip callers, not four duplicate detectors or a new registry.
`evidence-factories.ts` and the typed invoker/operand census gain the exact arms below together;
an existing symbol anchor is not proof that those callers consume the scoped source.

Reuse `PivotalKind` and only the family keys/kind validation of `PIVOTAL_MARKER_ROUTES`, not its
legacy exact route values. Keep that old map and whole-tip callers unchanged. Scoped dispatch
derives its exact versioned route from the table below: irreversibility@1 and the three @2
successors. Check exact route/kind equality before preparation; no @1 fallback or duplicate
detector registry. The constructor captures its closed fields, validates the
unchanged parser-admitted full run's original branch graph and **every original path FEN before
narrowing**, then resolves the requested endpoint and owns its exact prefix. Inherited endpoints
are valid, including one before that branch's own fork; comparison's distinct two-attempt rule
does not apply here. Unknown/off-path endpoints, malformed originals and extra fields refuse.
No detector, evidence factory or provider runs in construction. Admission is nominal and checked
at runtime; caller payloads, casts, spreads and JSON copies cannot act as sources.

The source retains an independent immutable parsed run image (or an already parser-owned immutable
image), `moduleDecisionStamp(run)`, captured branch/endpoint/kind and ordered original prefix nodes.
It must not retain writable caller backing records or freeze the caller's objects in place.
It is **recorded source scope only**, never storage
authenticity, current Inspector access or provider authority. Its runtime-internal reader is
owned by the four factories; server callers cannot provide replacement paths, markers, policy
pages or witness arrays. The server resolver grants this exact branch/prefix/family before
construction and the receiver compares those fields and current decision again before delivery.

Only irreversibility@1 gains its named new closed scoped arm, rejecting a genuine source of
another kind. D3841 replaces the earlier human-divergence@1 scoped arm with its explicit @2;
D3846 also requires phase_change@2 and option_collapse@2's ordered witness tuples. All old @1
whole-tip arms, declarations and receipt identities remain unchanged:

| Exact factory | Scoped input arm (sole arm for new @2) | Preparation and complete retained inputs |
|---|---|---|
| `derived.pivotal.irreversibility@1` | `{recordedPivotal: RecordedPivotalSource}` | Require kind `irreversibility`. Prepare only this family on granted incoming edges. Retain actual parent/child identities and FENs, committed move, the exact existing transition reading and recorded anchor position. A root cannot invent an edge. |
| `derived.pivotal.phase_change@2` | `{recordedPivotal: RecordedPivotalSource}` | Require kind `phase_change`; value-authority §2.3b's literal four operands. Walk only the granted prefix, including intervening unclear bands. Retain both actual definite readings and their recorded positions; the previous definite witness need not be the immediate parent. Never borrow a later phase or infer a transition from the displayed labels alone. |
| `derived.pivotal.human_divergence@2` | `{recordedPivotal: RecordedPivotalSource}` | Require kind `human_divergence`; use value-authority §2.3a. Preserve all actual selection observations anchored on the requested prefix, independently resolve each event's actual origin-branch run_node subject and seq, then mint its explicit recorded-selection and source-owned position inputs. Retain origin and requested branch/endpoint separately, plus stored requested target band. Never accept caller positions or a legacy provider policy-page input. |
| `derived.pivotal.option_collapse@2` | `{recordedPivotal: RecordedPivotalSource}` | Require kind `option_collapse`; value-authority §2.3b's literal six operands. Prepare only this family's same-side legal-count sequence within the grant. Retain prior, first-low and next same-side positions plus all three complete legal-mobility readings. The anchor stays the first-low node. A prefix missing the later witness produces no marker, even if that later node exists in the stored run. |

The existing @1 `{run, branchId}` arms and their whole-tip output, inputs and receipt identities
remain unchanged. Scoped factories do not call those arms, compute the combined population and
filter it, or manufacture a shorter DrillRun. They use their source's granted path before their
own preparation, then existing exact reading/position factories against the unchanged original
run. For irreversibility@1, keep current marker order, payloads, thresholds, conventions, grounding
and dependencies; a complete-tip source retains byte-equal payload populations to its legacy arm.
Phase_change@2 and option_collapse@2 preserve ordered marker payload populations, thresholds,
conventions and grounding, but publish the explicit four/six-input declarations from §2.3b;
their new scoped receipts do not rewrite old @1 receipts. Human-divergence@2 preserves the ordered marker population and
old marker-field image, thresholds and conventions, while explicitly adding the recorded
selection/position lineage and recorded_run grounding specified in value-authority §2.3a.
Its old @1 arm remains byte-identical. New source input identities never rewrite old receipts.
If independent review finds a v1 meaning requires complete tips, return for explicit successor
projections/bindings before acceptance rather than silently widening that meaning.

**Human occurrence is not selected-branch attribution.** Current v1 observes distributions at
recorded nodes: two branches can share an anchor while their selection events have distinct
origin branches, sequences and chosen moves. Preserve that population. Validate the origin
branch contains the anchor and the retained tuple/image equals its unique actual event; do not
require the event's chosen move to be a committed edge on the requested prefix. The endpoint
bounds position witnesses, not a historical event-time cut: observations come from the admitted
current full event head. A different chronological-cut feature requires its own contract.
Current read/disclosure permission remains separate and is never inferred from that anchor.

The historical human source's requested target band still comes from the sealed head's stored
`run.opponentPolicy.targetElo` (or null),
not an invented historical sample field. Retain that exact operand and limitation. Recorded
engine/candidate bytes and an event reference do not establish a modern Maia task receipt,
calibrated rating or best-move judgement. Its @1 provider dependency remains unchanged. The @2
recorded source requires independent acceptance and actual new source/factory adoption, not
relabelling. It does not discharge modern task availability or make that old dependency vanish.

Canonical factory inputs include `recorded-pivotal-source@1`, the exact requested route/kind,
original run/current decision, branch/endpoint, ordered granted node/edge operands and actual
used witness/selection records. Never digest only an opaque handle, displayed marker, run/node
pair or normalized masses. Distinct endpoints and repeated equal-image selection events remain
distinct inputs. Retain all declared reading/source inputs and weakest-input grounding; do not
change a convention or promote a legacy receipt to exact/provider-validated.

Successful `no_witness` means only **no complete qualifying witness inside this permitted
prefix**. It makes no assertion about the rest of the branch. An access refusal is withheld;
invalid stored source data is `failed/source_contract_invalid`, not successful absence. A held
source version keeps its exact registered dependency reason. Do not scan hidden later positions
to decide whether to advertise that a withheld witness exists.

These are synchronous, requested-family operations. Check the same AbortSignal before source
construction, before family/factory work and before publication. Outer asynchronous preparation
uses the resolver's existing post-await storage/access/assistance checks; the client separately
retires obsolete request owners and paint. No source handle authorizes an old response.

Acceptance requires actual typed constructor/new-arm/invoker controls, not the disposable helper:
irreversibility@1 and phase_change@2/option_collapse@2 full-tip marker payload populations retain
legacy bytes, with the new @2 four/six-input declarations and exact occurrence receipts; human@2 retains the
ordered old marker-field population plus its explicitly added recorded lineage, while old
human@1 stays byte-identical; all207 stored prefixes retain complete
family witnesses; later graph **and FEN** corruption refuse before detector counters advance;
copied/wrong-kind/wrong-scope sources and duplicate/missing/substituted human occurrences refuse;
mutating caller argument/node/event records after construction cannot alter the admitted image.
Repeated equal-image observations must have distinct new input identities while old-arm receipts
remain unchanged. Keep the four missing-later-collapse negatives and all94 stored occurrence
controls. Actual authenticated HTTP/client/desktop/phone requests must cover every family,
inherited endpoints, revocation, cancellation and late-response retirement. Current research
passes500 source controls plus828 family checks; its original-path FEN negative first fails,
then passes after repairing only the disposable bundle. Production source/factory/receiver and
native delivery remain unimplemented, and independent acceptance still gates all of them.

**D3813 bounded resolver/receiver author model — still unaccepted:**
`make inspector-recorded-context-research` exercises real IdentityService sessions, requireRead,
RunService.assistanceAuthority and file-backed SQLite over three legal branches. Forty-four
controls preserve the complete current run while granting a four-node prefix of a seven-node
main path, requested peer order and inherited-prefix selection. A real sole mobility-event
factory emits four source-bound events from the granted incoming edge. This is bounded author
evidence, not the proposed production resolver, all-source execution or HTTP/native proof.

The proposed **server-private** callable is
`RunService.resolveInspectorRecordedContext({runId, principal, requested, selection, signal})`.
Here `principal` comes from the existing authenticated request boundary; `requested` is the
existing RequestedAssistanceV1, not caller-supplied finalized authority; `selection` contains
exactly `branchId`, `nodeId`, `decisionDigest` and the already parsed source context above.
The service owns storage, current assistance origin/access and actual provider-health lookup.
It returns an admitted process-local context, a closed refusal, or the declared missing-context
result; it does not return chess text, provider values or a new public/stored token.

Resolver order is authentication/read access, current decision, authoritative assistance and
effective explicit Inspector effect, original graph validation for **all requested branches**,
then prefix extraction. Capture selection/context fields into independent immutable records;
retaining a mutable request reference is insufficient. The model's added negative first passes
a branch→recorded-window mutation through the receiver because both have the same path; copying
the fields makes that negative refuse. A frozen outer object alone does not freeze its context.
Position grants one selected node, incoming edge its actual parent/child, recorded-window a
contiguous prefix ending at selection, and branch its explicit through-node prefix containing
selection. Comparison grants ordered primary/peer prefixes after both reach their recorded forks;
the exact fork node is valid, a node before it is not an attempt. Source owners still validate
their complete legal witness ranges and versions. This does not create a new completed-Review
authority: D3824 models only its source boundary; current-access composition and complete Review
source assembly remain unmodelled here and owned by the Review and Inspector contracts.

The proposed request owner calls
`RunService.revalidateInspectorRecordedContext(context, {principal, requested, selection, signal})`
after every asynchronous source result and before response serialization. Reauthenticate through
the request's existing identity boundary, rederive run access/origin/assistance and validate the
current original paths; compare captured learner/role, full current decision, final assistance,
selection/context fields and granted occurrences. Forward the same AbortSignal to acquisition.
The server retains the original immutable parsed request, **not a new global current-selection
or preference store**. UI selection, preference and account changes that leave an old HTTP
connection open are not server-observable. The client must separately compare its existing
Inspector request owner, current selection/configuration and disclosure before consumption or
paint, even if the server returns a valid old-request response. The model's mutable `current`
callback simulates that owner boundary; it is not evidence the server knows unreported UI state.
Closed/disposed UI requests may retire consumption even without a server disconnect. This model
demonstrates suspended acquisition refusal after owner selection, same-path context, preference,
role, grant revocation, identity, logout, sibling event-head, disclosure or cancellation changes.
Client receiver/native paint tests remain separate obligations. It does not prove
live provider cancellation, response parsing, source-occurrence retention or browser delivery.
No source-attachment exception, synthetic run or re-labelled whole-tip receipt is admitted.

**D3814/D3815 recorded-window source-owner proposal — still unaccepted:** the earlier
disposable acquisition candidate compared `resolveBranchPath.kind` with `available` instead
of `resolved`. Four actual candidate-query controls now cover own/inherited/root/off-path
selection; the inherited-node control first fails on the wrong creating branch. Correcting
the instrument leaves its canonical ten-family reach result unchanged. It is not an explicit
endpoint supplier or production query repair.

The scoped recorded-window supplier is proposed as
`recordedSemanticWindowSource({run, branchId, throughNodeId, projection})`, a new runtime
helper owned by this amendment's source execution. `run` is the unchanged full stored image
retained by the admitted server context; branch/endpoint/projection are independently captured
primitive fields from the exact parsed request and grant. It accepts only the exact new scoped
evaluator image: trade-completed@2 and F2 §2.3j's ten recorded@3 rows, retaining the existing
family/horizon table (contact2/3, attraction3/5, other fixed horizons). Do not dispatch by latest
version, base id alone or unchanged `version===2`. It is **not** a new optional scope
argument silently added to `recordedSemanticPath`/its v1 result identity. All existing whole-tip
callers and historical result identities remain unchanged. F2 §2.3j owns the ten additive
projection/factory contracts and explicit direct-convention retention; they are not aliases for
old@2 values. No new convention definition, stored record, schema or provider protocol.

Runtime execution validates the original graph and endpoint, then legal-replays every edge in
the granted prefix through the existing `run.record.edge@1` factory with the original node
references. Exact projection admission precedes any edge factory. Preparing detector inputs is
a separate step: choose only the requested projection's complete evaluator rows **ending at
throughNodeId**, and prepare the union of their required trailing edges. Never prepare a later
edge, another family's row, every earlier window, or a full branch and then filter its result.
The present table needs at most five prepared edges; full graph/prefix validation is not thereby
bounded to five or exempted. Captures are prepared only for trade/deflection/attraction/checking
zwischenzug/overload-exploitation; check inputs only for deflection/attraction/checking zwischenzug.
Defender-duty/exchange inputs remain those rows' exact lazy source-owner dependencies. A family
with no complete row performs no detector preparation and has missing context, not no-witness.
For each complete @3 row, the source owner privately issues RecordedSequenceWindow with the
independently retained original source/grant, exact requested ref/horizon and genuine ordered
edge/dependency invocations. F2 §2.3j's sole factory arm authenticates it through the private
reader before computation/mint. Retain all0/1 capture results before ordered flattening for the
three complete-capture families and enforce the indexed check/duty/exchange joins. A caller
array or successful old@2 factory call does not establish this new owned source. Valid unmatched
windows are empty; absent/corrupt required sources refuse, not empty success.

The proposed private result is a closed union with distinct `window_available`, `window_missing`
and `window_refused` kinds, deliberately not assignable to the whole-path result. Available
contains exact `{runId,branchId,throughNodeId,projection}` scope, genuine events from the requested
exact successor (or unchanged trade@2),
complete `RecordedPathWindowReceipt`s, omitted-horizon numbers, existing convention closure and
one identity computed with the existing canonical digest function under the new operation
`recorded-semantic-window@1`. Its identity binds scope, exact completed/omitted row image and
event/source/convention closure. It is not a public token or new cache. Missing contains that
scope, `no_recorded_window`, required horizons and available-prefix edge count. Refused contains
only the existing original-graph/legal-edge reason or `endpoint_not_on_branch`/
`projection_unsupported` for graph/request failures. It additionally has an explicit private
source-failure variant: reason `source_contract_invalid | source_lookup_failed`, or reason
`source_version_held` with the required exact actual factory dependency. Missing/corrupt capture
execution, false membership/index joins, copied invocations/handles or a required factory
contract exception are source_contract_invalid, never malformed-graph or empty success. Actual
source-owner lookup failure is source_lookup_failed. A genuine typed version hold must satisfy
the existing exact dependency rule below; unresolved nested/mismatched dependencies are
source_contract_invalid, not a guessed hold. Private diagnostics never cross the Inspector wire.

The Inspector adapter maps missing to `missing_context/no_recorded_window`; available with
at least one complete window but no events to `no_witness`; and emitted events only through
their exact admitted module refs/presentation path. The generic compiler retains all eleven
families, but new Inspector admits its ten proposed@3 recorded successors, not the excluded trade
family. Existing whole-tip/Review emitters remain@2 unless separately migrated.
The ten v1 sequence obligations remain independently held; do not relabel their payloads as @3
or remove them. Other consumers, especially longitudinal opportunities, keep their own contracts.
The scope result never grades, ranks, selects useful moments or supplies hypothetical lines.
The adapter maps the two private source-failure reasons only to the same closed `failed` outcome,
and a genuine source_version_held only to `unavailable/source_version_held` with its exact owner.
It must not translate either into no_witness/source_absent. Existing graph/request refusals keep
their request-level refusal mapping; no dependency error string or private source details leave
the server. Exercise these boundaries even on a valid complete legal graph.

`make inspector-recorded-prefix-research` executes guarded in-memory source variants. The
unchanged baseline fails the exact zero-edge prefix control. The prefix variant passes 452
prefixes over 104 legal canonical/mirrored/castling runs and 312 original-graph refusals. The
requested-family variant matches existing complete event values/receipts over the same 452
requests, plus 264 requests across eight actual server-imported/stored games (513 edges).
It distinguishes 304 missing-context requests from 92 complete-but-empty requests and prepares
