at most five edges; seven/eight-ply examples do not cause whole-prefix detector preparation.
These measurements establish the algorithm's bounded source behavior, not compiled production
types, the proposed operation identity/result parser, authenticated Inspector source acquisition,
worker cancellation, source performance discharge or HTTP/component/native receiver proof.
The prototype uses unmistakable research-only identities, never production scope receipts.
The D3847 extension separately compiles prospective@3 fixed/anyOf declarations with unchanged
F1, matches actual recorded positive tuples and checks direct convention retention. It does not
run the proposed owned-window issuer or new factories: the source prototype above still emits
old@2 events. Keep that distinction in qualification and never relabel its diagnostic output.

Refuse wrong/off-branch/foreign endpoints and withheld spans before collector/provider reads.
After async acquisition or computation, recheck the **current full stored authority**, access,
effective configuration, decision, original graph endpoints and exact retained source occurrence.
The actual probe's SQLite write retires the old Review subject while its selected node still exists;
mere node existence or an unchanged FEN is not a current source/disclosure check. No stale waiver
is granted for a source attachment. Close, departure and changed scope retire pending publication
and board paint without deleting saved alternatives or source data.

Acceptance must exercise valid inherited prefixes, zero-length-at-fork and distinct comparisons,
late source arrivals (real owner-admitted sources, not mock authenticity), original/branched native
and imported completed boundaries, malformed original graphs, unavailable/withheld contexts,
source/config/access advances and cancellation at each receiver. The current probe proves storage/
runtime boundaries and stale-subject retirement only; authentication, live provider/late-source
admission, exact-endpoint source execution and production response/native proof remain open.

Missing required context is distinct from source absence and no-witness. Foreign/nonexistent or
off-branch IDs refuse without revealing another run's facts. This proposed grammar changes no
existing module query, source predicate, automatic selection or disclosure policy.

**Closed response and bounded paging (D3801 author image):** `inspector.query_page@1` has two
top-level alternatives. `refused` contains only `protocol`, `kind`, `code`, where code is exactly
`invalid_request | not_found | withheld | stale | unsupported_version`. No private run/branch/source
data accompanies a refusal. HTTP is 422/404/403/409/422 respectively; unsupported old deployments
remain the client's explicit unsupported-version state rather than fallback output.

`page` contains only `protocol`, `kind`, `runId`, `branchId`, `nodeId`, the existing
`ModuleDecisionStamp`, `requestedConfigDigest`, `effectiveConfigDigest`, `disclosure` and `body`.
The requested selection and current decision cursor are distinct and both retained. Body is:

| Body kind | Exact additional fields | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `inventory` | `range`, `topics[{projection,family,state}]` | Exact eligible registered topics; family is one of the existing eight IDs, state only `not_requested`; no source work/count claim |
| `source` | `projection`, `context`, `outcome`, `recordedCoverage`, mate-only `parameters` | One requested source, complete execution outcome; parameters required only for the two mate refs; coverage is exactly the ledger-specific closed object below or null |
| `detail` | `projection`, `context`, `sourceKey`, `scope`, `range`, `fullSourceTotal`, `sourceOrdinals`, `presentation` | One bounded batch of complete typed members |
| `detail_limited` | `projection`, `context`, `sourceKey`, `scope`, `ordinal`, `sourceOrdinal`, `selectedTotal`, `fullSourceTotal`, `exceeded` | A complete member cannot fit; no zero-progress page or silently cropped substitute |

Every range is exactly `{start,count,total,next}`. All numeric fields are nonnegative safe
integers, count at most eight, start/count inside total, and `next` is exactly `start+count`
when more remain, otherwise null. A successful nonempty population never returns zero count.
An empty population is only `{start:0,count:0,total:0,next:null}`. Over-budget individual members
use `detail_limited`, not a self-loop cursor. Source-order members never reorder by usefulness.

Source outcome is closed:

- `missing_context` has only `reason`, exactly `no_incoming_edge | no_recorded_window |
  no_second_attempt | no_recorded_review`.
- `unavailable` has two closed alternatives. For `source_version_held`, it has exactly
  `kind`, `reason` and required `dependency`: the exact registered pending source owner's RFC
  slug from that requested factory's `EvidenceValueFactoryMeta.dependency` (without `.md`).
  It cannot be `none`, empty, caller supplied, an error string or another source's owner.
  Source-owner acquisition must establish the actual version hold; pending metadata alone
  cannot turn a working computed source or qualification limit into unavailability. A returned
  factory availability's dependency must agree with that exact owner. An unresolved nested
  dependency without an authored source-specific mapping is `failed/source_contract_invalid`,
  not permission to guess or erase the blocker. Existing opening `artifact_missing` with
  `runtime-opening-identity` maps to this held outcome while that authority remains unlanded;
  source-owner migration, not this wire declaration, changes the underlying availability.
  All other unavailable alternatives have exactly `kind` and `reason`, with no dependency
  field: `source_absent | provider_unavailable | authored_source_absent | invalid_turn_clone |
  no_legal_recapture | recorded_measurement_unavailable | artifact_missing`.
  `no_legal_recapture` is admitted only for the strict overload projection above; neither
  invalid request horizons nor `budget_exhausted` are source-unavailability alternatives.
  `recorded_measurement_unavailable` is admitted only for the two ledger topics with the
  completed refused/abstention-only coverage below, never a missing or invalid artifact.
  artifact_missing is admitted only for theory.opening.current_endpoint@1,
  theory.opening.catalogue_membership@1 and derived.opening.deepest_reached@2 after the installed
  source owner actually establishes that state. Before source migration the existing old factory
  hold above remains honest; it is not the permanent result after successful installation.
  Each requires actual
  source-owner absence/refusal evidence; no universal stub satisfies acquisition obligations.
- `failed` has only `reason`, exactly `source_contract_invalid | provider_failed |
  source_lookup_failed | artifact_invalid | digest_mismatch`. The last two are admitted only
  for those same three opening topics, from actual installed-source admission. A bad history
  grant/invocation/value is source_contract_invalid, not an invented artifact failure. Broken
  bindings are failures, not chess absence. All three installed-source statuses have null
  recordedCoverage and no facts; the exact outcome is retained in sourceOutcome and verified
  body/receipt equality before fixed operational status rendering. No raw loader errors,
  private paths, catalogue rows, source handle or new provider attempt crosses this wire.
- `no_witness` has no additional fields and requires successful complete source execution.
- `below_floor` is a successful source-local status admitted only for
  derived.explorer.inspector_population@1. It has exactly kind, sourceKey, total and population.
  total is a safe integer in [0,99]. population has exactly ratingBuckets, speeds, since, until,
  copied from the actual admitted narrow value's canonical ExplorerPositionPageRequest; use
  that request's existing allowed bucket/speed/order/date/null rules, not a looser UI parser.
  The fixed minimum is100, not a caller threshold. This state contains no facts, presentation,
  recommendation, per-move counts, budget-deferred fields or source diagnostics.
- `available` has exactly `collectionKey`, `range`, `collected`, `displayed`, `deferred`,
  `facts` and `presentation`. Facts are bounded ordered occurrence summaries, each exactly
  `{sourceKey,disposition,exceeded,detail}`. Disposition is `presented | deferred`; exceeded
  dimensions are unique members of `facts | words | marks | arrows`. Detail is null or the
  exact one of six new view refs belonging to this source. No source payload/raw JSON travels
  in this summary. Presentation is the existing strictly parsed `PresentationReceipt`.

**D3858 sparse Explorer status — same unimplemented source operation.** Before any below_floor
status, perform the same complete consumer preflight, granted-node/source preparation, actual
whole-page delivery and narrow-factory admission as the >=100 arm. Check the complete normalized
request against that node's captured Explorer position/population/window and current authority;
Explorer's existing counter-neutral position normalization remains unchanged. A clock-equivalent
page may match, a sibling node/population/date window may not. Use no legacy stats fallback,
HTTP presentation receipt reconstruction or bare response object to establish this status.

The source-owning operation derives sourceKey through the same actual source-occurrence key
constructor as a normal Explorer fact: granted run/branch/node/context plus the genuine whole
page/narrow item and complete delivery identity. Equal page bytes at a foreign occurrence do
not authorize disclosure. It derives total and population from that same admitted narrow
value, not another page or request parameter copy. Below100 bypass only the >=100 fact adapter,
not source admission; invoking that adapter and swallowing its expected refusal is not this arm.
Totals0 and99 are settled sparse successes; at100 use the existing available fact path. The
population and100-per-move floors, canonical order/unlisted mass/attribution/recency behavior
on available pages and old inspector.corpus@1/@2 APIs remain unchanged.

recordedCoverage is null. range/collectionKey/detailProjection in disclosure are null and
sourceKeys/sourceOrdinals/componentDigests are empty; the full below_floor outcome, including
its sourceKey, total and population, is retained in sourceOutcome. This source identity is
not a presented fact or budget-deferred item and has no collection continuation. Initial
start0/null-collection is required; a nonzero continuation cannot produce this status.
The client requires exact body/disclosure equality and exact request/decision/context correlation
before paint. Source/config/access/selection changes retire stale completions under the normal
rules. A matching digest alone supplies neither source issuance nor permission.

The fixed status component states "Not enough games in this Lichess explorer population to
show a distribution (N; minimum 100)" and the actual rating buckets/speeds/date window. Do not
claim no human played it, no good move, missing provider or statistical suitability for other
consumers. No raw JSON, arbitrary prose, new evidence leaf, fact receipt, voice/LLM input or
countermeasure query is introduced. Keep the source panel settled without board shrinkage,
overflow or repeat fetching merely because the population is sparse. Presentation's companion
owns the fixed component; actual issuer/response/parser/native tests and joint acceptance remain.

**D3841 recorded-source coverage — same unimplemented wire, not a new evidence leaf.** Every
source body has the required recordedCoverage field, null except for the two exact ledger topics
after successful complete artifact enumeration. In their available/no_witness or
unavailable/recorded_measurement_unavailable outcomes it is exactly:

```ts
interface InspectorRecordedCoverage {
  readonly sourceKey: string; // exact owned complete ledger-source identity, not a caller key
  readonly matchedRecords: number;
  readonly admittedRecords: number;
  readonly notAdmittedRecords: number;
  readonly abstentions: {
    readonly out_of_range: number;
    readonly source_unavailable: number;
    readonly no_data_at_band: number;
    readonly licence_withheld: number;
  };
}
```

All seven counts are nonnegative safe integers, including safe sums;
matchedRecords=admittedRecords+notAdmittedRecords. Every matching abstention increments exactly
its existing closed reason. Count matching records independently of abstentions; a report plus
abstention is not deduplicated. This is constant-sized metadata, not an unbounded diagnostic list.
Original ordinals, individual dispositions, timestamps/source links and raw abstention detail stay
in the private source proof, not arbitrary wire prose. No records may be silently dropped before
counting. Available requires admittedRecords>0 and collected=admittedRecords. No_witness requires
all counts0. Recorded_measurement_unavailable requires admittedRecords0 and at least one refused
record or abstention. Other outcomes and every other projection require null; missing, extra,
negative, fractional, overflow, inconsistent or wrong-topic fields refuse. These parsers check
shape/coherence, not source issuance: the server must derive counts/sourceKey from the actual
retained private source and the client must bind the entire returned value to its receipt.

The source collection key binds recordedCoverage.sourceKey and all complete admitted reading
occurrences in original order. Every page of that collection repeats the same coverage; changed
source or counts yields stale, not continuation into a different report. The operational source
status renderer shows admitted/not-admitted and each positive abstention count beside valid
fact pages, including refused-only/abstention-only states. It is a fixed typed status component,
not a chess fact, advice, raw ledger JSON, arbitrary caption or input to external voice/LLM.
Do not encode source refusal as deferred; that word remains exclusively presentation budgeting.
The native bounded-layout proof must include the compact coverage block and mixed fact pages.

`collected` is the full collection's fact total and equals range.total; `displayed+deferred`
equals this page's count, not the full collection. The facts length equals range.count with
unique source keys. Presented occurrences have no exceeded dimensions; a budget-deferred
occurrence has nonempty exceeded dimensions. Components retain their registered whole-fact
ownership. A source with no supported bounded detail door stays explicitly limited; it cannot
be cropped or count as fully read. Detail range counts members; `sourceOrdinals` has exactly
range.count entries and retains each member's original source ordinal. Under target focus these
need not equal consecutive selected ordinals. Full-source total remains distinct from selected total.

Initial source reads use start zero and null collectionKey. Continuations require a nonempty
previous collection key and positive start, recomputed against the complete ordered occurrence
collection and current decision/context. A changed collection refuses stale; no token authorises
access. No new cache or paging state is persisted. Retained source reads reconstruct from their
actual source authorities. Any owner-authorised acquisition advancing the run first requires a
new current decision/refresh; it is not excused from stale checks. Actual acquisition paths and
their cancellation remain source-owner implementation obligations, not established by this grammar.

**Disclosure binding:** `inspector.disclosure_receipt@1` is exactly `protocol`, `runId`, `branchId`,
`nodeId`, `decisionDigest`, `requestedConfigDigest`, `effectiveConfigDigest`, `view`, `range`,
`collectionKey`, ordered `sourceKeys`, ordered `sourceOrdinals`, `detailProjection`, ordered
`componentDigests`, `sourceOutcome`, `recordedCoverage`, and `digest`. recordedCoverage equals the
source body's entire field, including sourceKey and all counts, or is null for every non-source
body. Include it in the canonical receipt body for available, no_witness and refused/abstained
ledger measurements alike; changing/removing the coverage changes the receipt. `sourceOutcome`
is null for inventory/detail/
limited-detail bodies. For source bodies it is the exact closed nonavailable outcome, including
the held dependency; an available outcome instead contributes exactly `{kind:"available"}`
because its page/collection/source/component identities are already bound by the fields above.
Use the existing canonical presentation-digest construction
over this namespace/body without digest. Bind the actual returned range/source occurrences as
well as the exact request view/context; a request cursor alone does not bind the returned page.
Inventory has null collection/detail, empty source/ordinal/component lists; absent/failed source
outcomes have null range/collection/detail and empty lists. Detail binds its one original source,
selected original ordinals and sole view ref. Limited detail binds its original source/ordinal
and has no component. Client verifies all response/request/receipt equalities and parses each
registered presentation receipt; server constructs them only through admitted source/renderers.
The client requires the body-derived outcome summary to equal this receipt field and validates
the held owner's exact source join; changing status, reason or dependency changes the existing
receipt digest. A validly shaped dependency does not establish source acquisition or permission.
No caller receipt or digest can supply access, facts or rendering authority.

Every final response rechecks access, effective configuration, decision and source context after
asynchronous work. Close, navigation, selection, branch, help or disclosure changes retire pending
completion and board paint. Old/new protocol mismatches, extra/missing nested fields, duplicate
occurrence keys, substituted source/context/range/component, wrong supported view and invalid
continuations refuse. Six exact payload/renderer schemas and actual authenticated/native response
admission still need independent acceptance; the disposable request/range checks do not prove them.

**Truthful states:** retain `not_requested`, `unavailable`, `failed` and `no_witness` as distinct
source outcomes. `no_witness` requires successful complete source execution with no admitted
witness; it cannot mean “fitter dropped it.” Explorer's exact below_floor status is a successful
source with a refused display population, not any of those failure/absence states. Available output distinguishes collected, displayed
and deferred fact counts. An actual budget-limited result carries the existing exceeded dimensions
and the specific source fact's detail door; it never says that source found nothing. An invalid
binding/factory contract is a typed operation failure, not chess absence. Focused legal-control
unavailability remains visible even beside available geometric control.

**Detail is derivation, not a cropped original fact:** add only these six literal projections,
each with its exact original source as a required input:

| New detail projection, version 1 | Sole source | Complete members |
|---|---|---|
| `derived.inspector.detail.rays` | `rules.tactic.reading.ray_classification@1` | One full ray, ray squares and optional material comparison |
| `derived.inspector.detail.square_control` | `rules.square.reading.control@1` | One typed controller/target relation or explicit empty target; legal availability retained |
| `derived.inspector.detail.piece_destinations` | `rules.mobility.reading.piece_destinations@1` | Origin/destination legal and local-exchange membership; complete availability/empty records |
| `derived.inspector.detail.destination_changes` | `rules.mobility.event.piece_destinations@1` | Piece context and destination membership in all eight retained before/after/gained/lost sets |
| `derived.inspector.detail.king_zone` | `rules.king.reading.zone_state@1` | Zone membership, attacker/defender relations, shelter and escape/availability members |
| `derived.inspector.detail.defender_duties` | `rules.tactic.reading.defender_duty_set@1` | Full defender/target duty including all co-defenders; explicitly geometric, not legal recapture advice |

Their six sole factories receive admitted complete sources plus the closed view scope/start,
compute their own members and retain their literal inputs. No generic source slicer, arbitrary
JSON path, caller text or “derived sentence” projection is admitted. Register these views only
to exact `module.full_inspector@2`, with the original source answer image, weakest grounding and
exactness inherited without promotion. They do not release any unqualified semantic subject or
bind to voice, Nudge, bots, skills or pack truth. `evidence-presentation` owns their closed
operand parsers, registered renderers and typed components; this section owns the operation,
demand, scope and lifecycle. Both contracts must be coherent before implementation.

Original scope/conventions/limitations also survive. In particular, square control's accepted
legal turn-clone rule clears en passant; it is not the actual complete legal-move map. The
proposed detail names that limit explicitly. D3797's legal en-passant counter-control demonstrates
why target focus must not silently upgrade a source proxy into full rules legality.

Each view holds at most eight **complete** members, stops before any existing fact/word/mark/
arrow limit, and labels its selected range, selected total and full-source total. A source or
member that cannot fit is explicitly limited; no arrow, caption, comparison or membership flag
is silently removed. `control_target` is legal only for the square-control view and requires
an exact square, side and mode. It means that target's controllers, not pawn advances, all moves
of a selected piece or a quality judgement. The complete original reading remains unchanged.
Other focus kinds require separate source-backed specification, not a generic field selector.

`sourceKey` is server-issued from the exact run/branch/node, projection and retained value/input/
source occurrence receipts. `start` is a non-negative safe integer selecting a member in this
specific source/scope; out-of-range, changed source or decision yields a typed stale/invalid
response before any continuation work. No paging state is persisted. Deterministic member order
is source order or explicitly declared square/set order, never usefulness/lift/prominence ranking.
No member may duplicate or disappear across continuations.

Response correlation uses the existing decision/effective-config authority and strict sealed
presentation parsing, but a distinct `inspector.disclosure_receipt@1` body must also bind the
exact source, scope, selected range and ordered component digests. Do not reuse the old receipt
namespace for a changed body. Recheck access/decision before publishing an async result; source
attachment that advances the recorded decision requires refresh, not a special stale waiver.
Close, selection, branch, help, source and disclosure changes retire pending views; accessibility
and board paint inherit the same ceiling. The existing modal/focus/board ownership remains.

**Acceptance remains red at HEAD:** prove all 75 real suppliers or an explicitly authorised
source retirement; the ten recorded positive/hard-negative families; all six complete detail
images and round trips; exact-scoped empties/unavailability; zero-work refusals and cancellation;
old/new protocol rejection/compatibility; forged plan/payload/text/component and cross-source/
scope/ordinal refusals; immutable saved play/source values; and actual authenticated application,
client parser, desktop/phone/keyboard open → choose → read → focus/page → reload → return.
The model's 134,144 control-focus cases are representation evidence, not those production checks.
No 1.0 completion or execution-pair promotion follows from this draft.

**Authoring still required before buildability acceptance:** turn the D3799 supplier inventory
into exact production acquisition/absence/receiver/cancellation obligations, including its held
provider versions; independent review of D3802's six literal payload/factory/renderer schemas
in evidence-presentation, with actual envelope/source/component admission controls; and independent
review of the newly authored source-context/response/paging/disclosure
grammar, including real source/response admission controls (not just request-shape tests).
The request and six-type boundary
above are a bounded draft, not an executable contract or authority to implement a guessed response.

#### 4.11b Draft D3984 — keep whole readings when no board witness can be drawn

**State: accepted for bounded implementation, 2026-11-02.** Independent decision is recorded
in `planning/learner-modules/compiler2-independent-review-2026-11-02.md` after the companion's
relation-overlay caption clarification. This is not complete source-page/API/UI or parent
acceptance. This narrowly amends the accepted Full Inspector2
presentation image, not its65 acquisition topics, source meanings, six detail projections,
wire outcomes, permissions or budgets. The accepted §4.11a/source contract remains in force.
Exploration evidence: `design/research/module-evidence-assembly.md` §Whole Inspector readings
without drawable witnesses; `presentation-inspector-source.test.ts`; dated independent D3984
return and subsequent bounded acceptance in the existing compiler2 review.

**A source reading is not a selected positive annotation.** A genuine nonempty occurrence
collection stays available even if a retained reading has no named/flagged member or no
drawable geometry. Retain that original occurrence's source projection, owner, source key,
ordinal, context and actual typed state. Never replace it with no_witness, source_absent,
below_floor, a fake over-budget deferred entry, an unowned question, a detail projection or
an empty square/zero-length relation. A genuinely empty *collection* keeps its source owner's
existing no_witness outcome. Source invalidity, unavailable input and missing context remain
distinct; rendering an existing reading's internal unavailable state does not claim its
calculation succeeded or grant new source authority.

**Closed whole-reading population.** Only the17 exact@1 refs below receive this construction.
The predicates are source-derived presentation predicates, not source filters or safety tests.
They retain every reading, including all empty and internally unavailable variants. No caller
Boolean, exception handler, projection prefix or arbitrary function chooses components.

| Exact source (`@1`) | Complete required statement operands / zero or unavailable case | Optional geometry, from the current witness recipe only |
|---|---|---|
| rules.tactic.reading.loose_piece | Existing flagged-piece operands when flags exist; otherwise count0 plus the original pieces.length, never “safe” | none |
| rules.tactic.reading.ray_classification | Existing five-kind ray operands; rays[] states count0 | square_set: actual slider/blocker/target squares |
| rules.tactic.reading.rook_on_seventh | Existing rook operands; rooks[] states count0 | square_set: existing rook/king/pawn witnesses |
| rules.tactic.reading.trapped_piece | Existing pieces when kind=pieces/nonempty; pieces[] states count0 only for kind=pieces; kind=abstained retains trapped_while_in_check with no count0 assertion | relation_overlay: actual distinct attacker→piece edges |
| rules.tactic.reading.back_rank | Existing susceptible kings/escapes/heavy operands, including nonempty on_back_rank cases; susceptible[] states count0; equal heavy square/target means already occupies, not “can move there” | relation_overlay: existing heavy→fileTarget edges with from!=to only |
| rules.tactic.reading.discovered_latency | Existing screen operands; screens[] states count0 | relation_overlay: actual screen→target edges |
| rules.tactic.consequence.threat | Existing threats when kind=threats/nonempty; threats[] states count0 only for kind=threats; kind=abstained retains pass_while_in_check without a count0 assertion | none |
| rules.tactic.consequence.mate_in_one | Existing mate operands; mates[] states count0 within this immediate legal-move reading only | none |
| rules.structural.reading.space | Existing color totals and all differential-zone operands, including zero totals; no whole-position space/safety inference | square_set: existing color-zone squares |
| rules.structural.reading.pawn_connectivity | Existing per-color island/pair/chain counts, including zeros | square_set: existing island squares |
| rules.phase.development | Existing White/Black home-square piece operands, including both empty lists; never “fully developed” | square_set: existing home-square members |
| rules.tactic.reading.promotion_pressure | Existing pawn operands; pawns[] states count0 for this pressure convention, not no future promotion | square_set: existing pawn/promotion squares |
| rules.mobility.reading.legal_moves | Existing turn, movable-piece and exact move counts, including0 | square_set: existing actually movable-piece origins |
| rules.mobility.reading.piece_destinations | Complete per-color availability plus available piece/legal/local-non-losing counts; an invalid_turn_clone color is not0 available moves | relation_overlay: existing available piece→legal destination edges |
| rules.pawn.reading.contacts | Existing contact/lock/passed/connected-passed-pair counts, independently retained even with contacts[] | relation_overlay: existing attacker→target contacts |
| rules.pawn.reading.candidate_majority | Existing candidate operands; candidates[] states count0 under the candidate-passer convention | square_set: existing candidate-pawn squares |
| rules.tactic.reading.defender_duty_set | Existing duty and distinct-defender counts, including0 | relation_overlay: existing defender→target duties |

For the14 geometric rows, the Full2 adapter's component order is mandatory fact_statement,
then optional square_set or relation_overlay. Its registered composition is
`inspector_whole_reading_geometry`; exactly one optional member has the single closed condition
`inspector_whole_reading_has_geometry`. The condition is permitted only for these14 exact
Full2 pairs. The statement owns list/panel forms; the optional geometry owns the exact
remaining forms of that pair. Static union equals the unchanged binding form set even when
an occurrence has no actual geometry. The three text-only rows remain single fact_statement.
No detail condition is broadened and no ordinary module/Full1 construction is changed by this
new composition. The obsolete Full1 table is still removed at actual coordinated cutover.

Extract statement operands independently of drawing; do not invoke an old witness-only
constructor and catch its exception as a source status. Reuse existing reviewed fixed
templates where they support exact zeros. The presentation companion below owns the finite
new empty/unavailable grammar and destination availability grammar. Whole-source construction
must check the genuine declared item and exact ref, and source-retention admission must
recompute the statement and geometry from that item. Source kind/reason are real operands:
correct the existing threat/trapped F1 operand declarations to include kind and optional reason
already present in their unchanged factory payloads. No new payload meaning, evidence version,
semantic convention, factory, data migration or independently allocated shared resource is added.

Keep the complete owned statement+geometry bundle atomic, charged once per original source
occurrence using the unchanged shared budgets. Do not crop operands, subtract words/marks,
split captions, change policies or remove a family to fit. Zero geometry costs zero geometry;
the real statement still costs its actual equivalent words. Deferred entries require real
exceeded dimensions; all-deferred source-order paging still advances. Detail payloads and
their independently accepted conditional composition retain their own exact contracts.

**Required falsifiers and completion boundary:** genuine factories for all17 rows, zero and
positive cases, both learner colors where the source carries color; trapped/pass-threat
abstention distinct from calculated zero; contacts[] with nonzero passed-pair data; a nonempty
already-on-back-rank source with no invented arrow; per-color clone-unavailable destinations;
copied/crossed source, forged count/state/reason/geometry/caption/owner and omitted/extra
components refuse. Ordinary-module/positive source semantics remain unchanged. Test real
whole bundles under budget, including a combined-overflow case with zero partial output.
Then actual source→complete Full2 consumer/compiler→wire receiver must preserve original
source key/projection/ordinal for a presented text-only reading and budget-deferred reading.
Constructor/parser tests alone may clear only this prerequisite, never D3792/API/UI/native
or parent/module completion. This amendment is accepted only after independent review of
both RFC clauses and their exact source/renderer/form/retention contracts.

#### 4.11d D4056 — explicit historical Inspector target and help

**State: accepted for implementation, independent review recorded in
`planning/learner-modules/compiler2-independent-review-2026-11-02.md`.** This closes D3999's missing UI reach, not
a new disclosure policy. The existing server-owned `resolveInspectorRecordedContext` admits a
disclosed imported/completed line while active retry help is closed. Ordinary `/assistance`
confirms active Play only; §4.11c/Review §7a's `review` envelope confirms an incoming edge and
correctly refuses roots. Neither is permission to borrow active help or omit historical roots.
Evidence: `inspector-recorded-context.test.ts`'s completed/imported disclosure control and
`review-analyze-confirmation.test.ts`'s source-free/root refusal controls,63/two passing3.12s.

**One source-free arm of the existing endpoint.** `POST /runs/:id/assistance` additionally accepts
exactly this closed body, with no new route, protocol/resource version or persisted schema:

```ts
{
  assistance: RequestedAssistanceV1;
  inspector: { branchId: string; nodeId: string; decisionDigest: string };
}
```

IDs are nonempty strings and the decision digest has its existing exact grammar. Unknown,
accessor, extra, crossed or combined `review`/`inspector` fields refuse. The sole original
requested-assistance parser is `parseInspectorRequestedAssistance`, whose own-data snapshot
precedes the existing intent parser; never pass untrusted intent straight to the latter.
The sole original Inspector context owner resolves the exact target with `context: {kind: "position"}`; a root
is a real position, not an invented incoming edge. Current authentication/read role, branch
disclosure, rated/live locks, workflow context and Full2 effect apply. Complete74-binding
preflight and current provider-health availability apply as for the existing demand operation.
Re-read the original context before publication and honor cancellation. Do not change ordinary
active-Play confirmation, Analyze's edge/retry rules or any source-page permission.

Success contains exactly `{assistance, inspector}`, where assistance is the actual finalized
receipt from that owner and inspector is exactly `{runId, branchId, nodeId, fen, decision}`
from the actual selected position/current decision.
The historical branch/node may intentionally differ from `decision.cursor`: the stamp is
the actual current full-run decision, never a synthetic target-cursor decision.
No caller FEN/role/permission, source,
capture, raw run, evidence or query result crosses this confirmation. It invokes no source,
factory, provider parser/acquisition, analysis enqueue or storage writer. It is not a reusable
grant token: every later source-page request still performs its original current authorization.
Existing HTTP errors remain typed errors; no raw failure text becomes learner prose.

**Actual client and explicit advanced journey.** Add a strict client operation for this arm,
leaving ordinary `assistance()` and Review Analyze unchanged. At the Advanced Inspector, offer
an explicit historical line/position selector and an action to open its recorded evidence,
including roots and inherited-prefix positions. It does not switch the active branch/cursor,
reveal current Play, modify stored preferences or acquire evidence before that action. Use an
in-memory explicit Analysis preference for this requested historical inspection, under the
actual current workflow context; its ordinary role/workflow ceiling still applies. Describe
the separation visibly: this is recorded evidence for the selected line, not active retry help.
An undisclosed selected line remains a visible withheld/failure result, never a local grant.

The strict receiver matches the exact requested run/branch/node/FEN/current decision and
requested receipt/context before mounting `InspectorSourceBrowser` with those confirmed target
and help props. Its own-data snapshot and closed-record checks cover every nested finalized-help
record (config, permission, effects, suppressions, hint ceiling, source authority and optional
Campaign receipt), as well as the envelope and decision. Then the existing finalized parser
checks its established digest/domains. In particular an effect must match a declared registry
effect exactly; extra effect fields or a forged Full Inspector effect do not pass merely because
the public final digest was recomputed. This is receiver validation, not a second assistance
compiler or a claim that the generic finalized parser already closes those records.
It requires the finalized Full Inspector review effect; it never substitutes
the active branch, current board, a guessed ancestor or active Play's finalized digest. Reuse
the existing demand browser/source/detail controls, exact source-page grammar, source health
refresh and original peer endpoints. Selecting another target first retires pending confirmation,
pages/paint and ready state. Changes to the actual run snapshot, role/context/access or source-
health identity, close and teardown likewise abort/retire them; a matching decision digest alone
cannot preserve an old run snapshot. No late response may reopen a retired target. Duplicated
in-flight actions deduplicate only that exact target; later explicit retry remains available.
An explicit source-health refresh re-confirms this historical target/help, not active Play's
settings, and retires old pages while confirming. Pending, unavailable/withheld, retry and close
states are visible. Target changes/close preserve meaningful owned focus without stealing moved
focus; actual board actions remain usable after leaving the historical seat. The normal active
Inspector door keeps its existing gates and is not replaced by this separate explicit journey.

**Acceptance controls.** Authenticated/source-free tests cover genuine disclosed root/nonroot
targets/both colors, imported/completed history while retry help is closed, unchanged active
assistance/storage, current roles/revocation/rated/context/refusal/cancellation/stale/off-branch
states, exact closed envelopes and zero source/factory/provider work.
Current Campaign controls use the actual issued origin/receipt and `assistanceAuthority`,

closed retry, current-help noninterference, late/replaced/closed/teardown results, pending/retry/
health refresh and owned focus/real board input. Normal Make software/types/build/governance and
